| RWB-E1-001 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Recovery tables for the 14 data products | As a data steward and data product owner (RW-DATASTEW), I want the 92 recovery tables for the 14 data products created in the ACE database, with a data dictionary that covers those tables and the 30 read-only ACE views, and one system of record and one owner per data product, so that every Workbench record has one home and every data product has one accountable owner and one read path. | REQ-RDF-001 | Must | RWB-E1-044, RWB-E1-043 | data_product, sys_objects, sys_fields, party_ext, contact_point_ext, | RWB-E1-002 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Case list v1 with case ID, owner and queue | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want one case list with case number, account, product, stage, status, owner, queue, route, total balance and days past due for every account at 90 days past due or more, built from the open cases and the ACE account view on each ACE business date and available in Power BI, so that every recovery team works from one list that is dated to the ACE batch it was built from. | REQ-RDF-007 | Must | RWB-E1-018, RWB-E1-020, RWB-E1-069, RWB-E1-007, RWB-E1-015 | recovery_case, case_account, queue, v_ace_account, v_ace_efs_action, v_ace_batch_status, case_list_row | SCR-02 | MSG-P1-001, MSG-P1-011, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002 | 11 | 11 | D6 entry; D-RCV-35 (U1, U4); ADR-010 |
| RWB-E1-007 | E1 | P2 Integration | Integration adapters for outbound files | As a platform administrator (RW-PLATADM), I want one adapter pattern for every outbound file, with the data it reads taken from the ACE views, retry on a schedule held as data and an exception queue with a named owner, so that every later outbound file follows one pattern and a failed file is found on the day it fails. | REQ-DRI-006, REQ-DRI-007, REQ-DRI-008 | Must | RWB-E1-001, RWB-E1-069, RWB-E1-015 | interface_config, outbound_item, processed_event, outbox_event, outbox_delivery, v_ace_contact, v_ace_account, v_ace_batch_status, notice, document, debt_sale_account, recovery_case, task, role | SCR-09, SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-GEN-005, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-001, MSG-P2-001, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003 | 7 | 8 | D-RCV-35 (U1, U4); ADR-010 |
| RWB-E1-008 | E1 | P7 Decision center and log | Decision log store v1, append only and hash chained | As a center of excellence analyst (RW-COE), I want an append-only decision log store holding the seven field groups of every decision, with each entry hashed to the one before it, so that every decision from Epic 1 is recorded once, cannot be changed or deleted, and any change to a stored entry is found by recomputing the chain. | REQ-DRI-001, REQ-DRI-002, REQ-DRI-003 | Must | RWB-E1-001 | decision_log_entry | SCR-38 | MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-003, MSG-P7-008 | 8 | 10 | None |
| RWB-E1-011 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Reconciliation and data quality report | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want a daily reconciliation of the case list to `v_ace_account`, of every business key on a Workbench table to its ACE view, and of treatments and payments to their ACE records, with the variance by product and the data quality results in one Power BI report, so that everyone sees whether today's case list and its links can be trusted. | REQ-RDF-007, REQ-RDF-008 | Must | RWB-E1-002, RWB-E1-015, RWB-E1-069 | v_ace_account, v_ace_plan, v_ace_payment, v_ace_customer, v_ace_asset, v_ace_contact, v_ace_batch_status, case_list_row, recovery_case, case_account, case_party, case_entry_exclusion, treatment, proceeds, listing, asset_sale, debt_sale_account, notice, interaction, document, signature_request, reconciliation_result | SCR-45 | MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-001, MSG-P1-012, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002 | 8 | 9 | D-RCV-35 (U4); ADR-010 |
| RWB-E1-013 | E1 | P9 Reporting | Baseline measures | As the Tribe lead (RW-TRIBE), I want cases by route, age and queue, realized recovery, time in stage and turnaround per route published as the baseline, with definitions agreed by the Tribe, so that every later epic is measured against the same baseline. | REQ-RDF-012 | Must | RWB-E1-029, RWB-E1-018 | recovery_case, queue, case_account, v_ace_payment, measure_definition, measure_snapshot | SCR-37, SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P9-001, MSG-P9-003 | 8 | 9 | None |
| RWB-E1-014 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Decision log copy to the data lake | As a center of excellence analyst (RW-COE), I want the decision log copied to the data lake each night, with a retention rule applied to the copy, so that reporting reads the log without touching the live log and nothing in the copy is kept longer than its rule. | REQ-DRI-009, REQ-RDF-013 | Should | RWB-E1-056, RWB-E1-017 | decision_log_entry, lake_copy_watermark, retention_rule | SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-019, MSG-P2-006, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003 | 8 | 9 | None |
| RWB-E1-015 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Freshness stamp per data product | As a data steward and data product owner (RW-DATASTEW), I want every data product to carry the business date, start time, completion time and status of the ACE batch it came from, exposed to every consumer and shown on the case record view, and every daily data job to start only when the sources it reads are complete for the business date, so that every decision input states how old it is, stale facts are visible, and no list is built from a half-loaded day. | REQ-RDF-009 | Must | RWB-E1-001, RWB-E1-069, RWB-E1-027 | v_ace_batch_status, data_product, data_product_load, decision_log_entry, case_list_row | SCR-03, SCR-45 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-001, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-006 | 11 | 12 | D-RCV-35 (U1, U4); ADR-010; DL43 open |
| RWB-E1-016 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Access control per data product and catalog entries | As a data steward and data product owner (RW-DATASTEW), I want read access to each data product granted by role, source data reached only through the ACE view grant, personal fields masked by role from `v_ace_customer` and `v_ace_contact`, every read logged, and a catalog entry per product that names its owner, system of record, freshness, views and tables and fields, so that personal data is read only by the roles that need it and every product is findable. | REQ-RDF-010, REQ-RDF-011 | Must | RWB-E1-037, RWB-E1-001, RWB-E1-064, RWB-E1-069 | v_ace_customer, v_ace_contact, v_ace_account, party_ext, contact_point_ext, account_ext, valuation_ext, sys_objects, sys_fields, data_product, retention_rule, data_product_access, masking_rule | SCR-03, SCR-47 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-004, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-002, MSG-P1-003, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003 | 11 | 12 | D-RCV-35 (U4); ADR-010 |
| RWB-E1-017 | E1 | P1 Recovery data engine | Retention and archive rule for the data store | As a Compliance and PDPA officer (RW-COMPLY), I want a retention rule per data product and table, held as reference data, applied by a job that archives rows past their rule and logs the action, so that the store holds what the policy allows and nothing older. | REQ-RDF-013 | Should | RWB-E1-001, RWB-E1-043, RWB-TE-012, RWB-E1-064 | recovery_case, interaction, retention_rule | SCR-47 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-002, MSG-GEN-004, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-002, MSG-P1-020, MSG-P2-006, MSG-P4-002 | 9 | 10 | None |
| RWB-E1-018 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Case ID issuance and case record v1 | As a product owner, I want every account at 90 days past due or more in the ACE account view that is not excluded placed on one recovery case per customer and product, with a case ID in the form RW-YYYY-NNNNNN issued at creation and never reused, a status and its linked accounts read from ACE by business key, created by rule when the ACE ALS batch for the business date is complete, so that every team, letter and document refers to one case ID. | REQ-CWC-001, REQ-CWC-002, REQ-CWC-003, REQ-CWC-004, REQ-CWC-005, REQ-CWC-016, REQ-CHO-004 | Must | RWB-E1-001, RWB-E1-016, RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-042, RWB-E1-069 | recovery_case, case_account, case_party, case_entry_exclusion, v_ace_account, v_ace_customer, v_ace_ca_relation, v_ace_batch_status, party_ext, account_ext, rule_row, lov_value, decision_log_entry | SCR-02, SCR-03 | MSG-P1-001, MSG-P3-025, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-003 | 14 | 15 | D6 entry; D-RCV-35 (U1, U4); ADR-010; DL43 open |
| RWB-E1-019 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Roles, teams and queues as data | As a reference data administrator (RW-REFADM), I want queues, queue membership and capacity, each tied to a team code and a role code, held as versioned reference data loaded from the Excel template, so that the organization changes without a code release. | REQ-CWC-006, REQ-ACA-002 | Must | RWB-E1-038, RWB-E1-043 | queue, queue_member, reference_version, reference_row, role, lov_value, recovery_case | SCR-42 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-002, MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P1-002, MSG-P3-015, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-002 | 12 | 13 | None |
| RWB-E1-020 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Allocation and assignment engine v1 | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want every new recovery case placed in a queue and given an owner by the allocation and assignment rule tables on the morning it arrives, so that each case at 90 days past due has one owner from its first day and nobody allocates cases by hand. | REQ-CWC-007, REQ-CWC-008, REQ-CWC-009, REQ-CWC-016 | Must | RWB-E1-018, RWB-E1-019, RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-056 | recovery_case, queue, queue_member, assignment, rule_row, decision_log_entry | SCR-02, SCR-07, SCR-09 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P1-001, MSG-P3-011, MSG-P3-012, MSG-P3-013, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003 | 13 | 15 | D6 entry, take over and own the case |
| RWB-E1-021 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Transfer rules and engine v1 | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want cases moved between queues and roles by the transfer rule table on stage change, route decided, timer expired, deviation raised or cleared, approval needed, document received, payment missed, or a manual request with a reason, so that hand-offs between teams run without email. | REQ-CWC-010, REQ-CWC-016 | Must | RWB-E1-028, RWB-E1-020, RWB-E1-027 | recovery_case, queue, assignment, rule_row, decision_log_entry | SCR-02, SCR-03, SCR-05 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P3-001, MSG-P3-002, MSG-P3-005, MSG-P3-011, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-003 | 13 | 16 | None |
| RWB-E1-022 | E1 | P4 Access and administration | Sign-on with SSO, MFA and the first roles | As a case owner (RW-CASEOWN), I want to sign in to the Workbench once through Entra ID with multi-factor authentication and see only the screens and actions my Workbench roles allow, so that access is controlled from the first release and nobody holds a Workbench password or a local account. | REQ-ACA-001, REQ-ACA-002, REQ-ACA-010 | Must | RWB-E1-001, RWB-E1-019 | role, permission, role_conflict | SCR-43 | MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-005, MSG-P4-006 | 9 | 10 | None |
| RWB-E1-023 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Case record view v1, read only | As a case owner (RW-CASEOWN), I want one read-only screen per case with the case ID, accounts, arrears, owner, queue, stage, status, contact history and documents, read live from the ACE views by business key with the business date of each, so that I stop searching files and systems to understand a case. | REQ-OCC-007, REQ-RDF-009, REQ-ACA-005, REQ-ACA-006 | Must | RWB-E1-018, RWB-E1-024, RWB-E1-037, RWB-E1-039, RWB-E1-068, RWB-E1-069 | recovery_case, case_account, case_party, v_ace_account, v_ace_customer, v_ace_contact, v_ace_efs_action, v_ace_batch_status, interaction, document, queue | SCR-02, SCR-03 | MSG-P1-001, MSG-P3-004, MSG-P3-023, MSG-P3-024, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-003 | 12 | 13 | D-RCV-35 (U1, U4); ADR-010; DL43 open |
| RWB-E1-024 | E1 | P5 Document hub | Document store and index | As a litigation officer (RW-LITOFF), I want every document stored against its case with its type, date, source and extracted fields, legal types held in a write-once container, and every version kept, so that I find a document by case ID, type and date, and a legal document cannot be changed after it is stored. | REQ-DCM-001, REQ-DCM-002, REQ-DCM-003, REQ-DCM-008 | Must | RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-018, RWB-E1-025, RWB-TE-012, RWB-E1-001 | recovery_case, document, document_field, document_type, document_hub_parameter, decision_log_entry | SCR-30, SCR-32 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P5-001, MSG-P5-002, MSG-P5-006, MSG-P5-017, MSG-P5-025, MSG-P7-003 | 12 | 14 | D7.2 |
| RWB-E1-025 | E1 | P5 Document hub | Document types and fields agreed | As a center of excellence analyst (RW-COE), I want the first six document types (demand letter, court notice, appraisal report, title deed, settlement agreement, guarantor notice) with their key fields agreed with Legal and NPA and held as versioned reference data, so that the OCR pipeline, classification and extraction have one signed target list. | REQ-DCM-004 | Must | RWB-E1-069 | document_type | SCR-40 | MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P3-027, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-002 | 7 | 8 | D7.2 |
| RWB-E1-026 | E1 | P5 Document hub | Attach and search screen | As a case owner (RW-CASEOWN), I want to upload, see and search the documents of a case from the case record view, so that the case file is complete and I find a document by type and date without leaving the case. | REQ-DCM-008, REQ-DCM-001 | Must | RWB-E1-025, RWB-E1-024, RWB-E1-041, RWB-E1-045, RWB-E1-030 | recovery_case, document, document_type, document_hub_parameter, decision_log_entry | SCR-03, SCR-30 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-003, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P5-001, MSG-P5-006, MSG-P5-016, MSG-P5-017, MSG-P5-024, MSG-P7-003 | 11 | 13 | None |
| RWB-E1-027 | E1 | P7 Decision center and log | Decision log writer v1 | As the case workflow module (SY-CASEWF), I want one writer that validates and records case created, owner assigned, case transferred and document attached, with the rule ID and version used, in the same transaction as the action, so that the first decision records exist in Epic 1 and no action proceeds without its record. | REQ-DRI-004, REQ-DRI-005 | Must | RWB-E1-008, RWB-E1-056 | decision_log_entry, lov_value | None | MSG-P3-013, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-003, MSG-P7-009 | 7 | 9 | None |
| RWB-E1-028 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Service levels, timers, escalation and prescription dates | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want a service level per queue and status, a timer on every case, a warning to the owner and a breach raised to the named escalation point by rule, and a prescription date set from the legal periods, so that no case waits past its limit unseen and no legal claim expires unnoticed. | REQ-CWC-011, REQ-CWC-012, REQ-CWC-016 | Must | RWB-E1-045, RWB-E1-020, RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-043 | recovery_case, case_account, v_ace_account, queue, timer, reference_row, decision_log_entry | SCR-03, SCR-09 | MSG-P3-003, MSG-P3-004, MSG-P3-026, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-003 | 11 | 13 | D7 |
| RWB-E1-029 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Rule load, validation and rollback | As a center of excellence analyst (RW-COE), I want an Excel template for the five rule tables, validation on load (schema, overlaps, unreachable rows, missing default, 50 test cases and a dry run), a load with a version, approval, rollback by one call and a change log, so that a rule change goes from template to production without a code release. | REQ-CWC-013, REQ-CWC-014, REQ-DRI-004 | Must | None | rule_row, reference_version, recovery_case, lov_value | SCR-39 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-003, MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P3-015, MSG-P3-027, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-002 | 12 | 13 | None |
| RWB-E1-030 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Daily rebalancing and queue measures | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want a daily job that assigns unassigned cases and moves the newest cases off owners above the cap by the assignment rules, and queue counts, ageing and service level breaches published to Power BI, so that queues stay balanced and every team manager sees the same numbers as the case list. | REQ-CWC-015, REQ-RDF-012 | Must | RWB-E1-020, RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-028 | recovery_case, queue, queue_member, assignment, timer, queue_measure, decision_log_entry | SCR-02, SCR-07, SCR-09 | MSG-P3-011, MSG-P3-028, MSG-P3-029, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-003 | 10 | 12 | None |
| RWB-E1-031 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Queue and owner on the case list with filters | As a team manager (RW-TEAMMGR), I want the queue and owner columns on the case list, with filters by queue, team and status and an export to Excel, so that I see my queues and who owns each case without a new screen. | REQ-CWC-007, REQ-ACA-009 | Must | RWB-E1-020, RWB-E1-039 | recovery_case, queue | SCR-02, SCR-07 | MSG-P3-020, MSG-P3-021, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-003 | 8 | 9 | None |
| RWB-E1-032 | E1 | P5 Document hub | Automatic attach from the OCR intake | As a case owner (RW-CASEOWN), I want documents from the OCR intake attached to their case automatically when the case ID or the account number is read, with unmatched or ambiguous documents held for a person who sees the OCR text and the candidate cases and attaches the document, so that no document is filed by hand when the case is clear and none is filed on the wrong case when it is not. | REQ-DCM-007 | Must | RWB-E1-024, RWB-E1-050, RWB-E1-053 | recovery_case, case_account, v_ace_account, document, document_intake, document_text, decision_log_entry | SCR-30, SCR-31 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P5-003, MSG-P5-006, MSG-P7-003 | 15 | 17 | None |
| RWB-E1-033 | E1 | P5 Document hub | Search across cases | As a litigation officer (RW-LITOFF), I want a search across all cases in my scope by document type, date range, extracted field and free text, with every search written to the access log, so that I find a document in seconds without opening cases one by one, and every look at personal data can be audited. | REQ-DCM-001, REQ-ACA-006 | Should | RWB-E1-024, RWB-E1-053, RWB-E1-049 | recovery_case, document, document_field, document_text, document_type, document_hub_parameter | None | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-002, MSG-GEN-003, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P5-019, MSG-P5-020, MSG-P5-021 | 10 | 11 | None |
| RWB-E1-034 | E1 | P6 Communications | Letter template management | As a litigation officer (RW-LITOFF), I want a screen to upload a template version, have Legal counsel record the approval and set it current, so that templates are managed without a developer. | REQ-OCC-002 | Should | RWB-E1-046, RWB-E1-015 | template, merge_field | SCR-33 | MSG-GEN-001, MSG-GEN-003, MSG-GEN-004, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P3-005, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P6-003, MSG-P6-005, MSG-P6-006, MSG-P6-010, MSG-P7-002 | 10 | 11 | D7.1 |
| RWB-E1-035 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Case record view v1.1 with documents and history timeline | As a case owner (RW-CASEOWN), I want a timeline on the case record view with contacts, documents, assignments, transfers and status changes in time order, each with who or which rule caused it, so that I see what happened to the case without asking anyone. | REQ-CWC-016, REQ-OCC-007 | Should | RWB-E1-021, RWB-E1-023, RWB-E1-027 | interaction, assignment, document, decision_log_entry | SCR-03, SCR-04 | MSG-P3-023, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003 | 7 | 8 | None |
| RWB-E1-037 | E1 | P4 Access and administration | Role to permission matrix as data and PDPA field masking by role | As a product owner, I want the mapping of roles to capability areas, the scope of each role and the masking of each personal data field held as versioned tables that every screen and API applies, with the identity number masked inside the ACE view and contact values masked at the API, so that a change of rights is a data load with an approver, a person sees only the cases in their scope, and personal data shows unmasked only to roles that need it. | REQ-ACA-003, REQ-ACA-005, REQ-ACA-007, REQ-ACA-009, REQ-ACA-011 | Must | RWB-E1-022, RWB-E1-043, RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-069 | permission, role, masking_rule, v_ace_customer, v_ace_contact, recovery_case, decision_log_entry | SCR-43, SCR-47 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P1-002, MSG-P4-001, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-002, MSG-P7-003 | 13 | 15 | D-RCV-35 (U1, U4); ADR-010 |

| RWB-TE-006 | E1 | P2 Integration | Outbox and retry for outbound work | As a squad member (RW-TRIBE), I want every application event and every outbound work request written to an outbox in the same transaction as the change, and delivered to each consumer in order with retry, so that no outbound action is lost when a system or a consumer is down, and nothing is sent for a change that did not commit. | REQ-DRI-007 | Must | RWB-E1-044, RWB-E1-027, RWB-E1-007 | outbox_event, outbox_delivery, task, decision_log_entry, recovery_case | SCR-09, SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-005, MSG-P2-001, MSG-P2-022, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003, MSG-P7-003 | 11 | 13 | None |
| RWB-TE-007 | E1 | P3 Case and workflow | Scheduled jobs on Quartz | As a platform administrator (RW-PLATADM), I want the timer check, the morning allocation run and the daily reconciliation run as Quartz jobs with a run book each, so that scheduled work runs once, on time and visibly, and a missed run is caught up for its business date. | REQ-CWC-007, REQ-CWC-011, REQ-RDF-007 | Must | RWB-E1-044, RWB-E1-056 |  | SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P3-030, MSG-P3-031, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P4-003 | 10 | 11 | None |
| RWB-TE-008 | E1 | P7 Decision center and log | Decision log hash chain check | As an internal auditor (RW-AUDIT), I want a nightly job that recomputes the whole decision log hash chain, records the result with the last hash, and reports a break to Compliance the same day, so that a changed entry is found the day it happens and the log can be relied on in audit. | REQ-DRI-003 | Must | RWB-E1-008, RWB-TE-007 | decision_log_entry | SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-004, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-007, MSG-P7-008, MSG-P7-013 | 8 | 9 | None |
| RWB-TE-011 | E1 | P2 Integration | PDPA lint for code, tests and logs | As a squad member (RW-TRIBE), I want a pipeline check that finds personal data in code, test fixtures, log statements and error messages, built from the data dictionary's list of personal fields, so that personal data never leaks into code or logs. | REQ-RDF-010, REQ-ACA-005 | Must | RWB-TE-001 | v_ace_customer, party_ext, v_ace_contact, interaction, document_field | None | MSG-P4-002 | 10 | 11 | None |
| RWB-TE-012 | E1 | P5 Document hub | Document storage containers | As a platform administrator, I want blob containers for documents, with a write-once container for legal types, reached by managed identity, so that legal documents cannot be overwritten. | REQ-DCM-003, REQ-DCM-002 | Must | RWB-TE-001 | document | SCR-30, SCR-44 | MSG-GEN-006, MSG-P4-002, MSG-P5-002 | 12 | 13 | DL27 |
| RWB-TE-013 | E1 | P10 Agent runtime | Agent runtime client with fallback | As a platform administrator (RW-PLATADM), I want one client in the recovery module that calls the agent orchestrator, applies the autonomy mode, runs shadow mode and falls back to the rule result, so that agents are added to a case step without changing the case flow, and every step records what the agent did. | REQ-DRI-001, REQ-DRI-005, REQ-ACA-010 | Must | RWB-TE-007, RWB-E1-058, RWB-E1-027 | recovery_case, rule_row, decision_log_entry, agent_policy, agent_run | None | MSG-P4-002, MSG-P7-003 | 8 | 10 | DL33 |

| RWB-E1-001 | Recovery tables for the 14 data products | Feature: Recovery tables for the 14 data products<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Schema deployed<br>    Given the migration V0001__rwb_e1_001_recovery_schema.sql and the parameter recoverySchema = "recovery"<br>    When the pipeline applies it in DEV and then in SIT<br>    Then the recovery schema in each environment holds 92 tables: the 88 Workbench tables and the four extension tables party_ext, contact_point_ext, account_ext and valuation_ext<br>    Then it holds none of the tables that left the schema: loan_account, account_payment, party, contact_point, collateral, account_collateral, collections_history, party_product, valuation, score, asset, load_run, dq_exception, dq_rule, source_field_map, source_code_map, interface_run, interface_signoff, tracker_inventory and tracker_row<br>    Then no constraint in schema recovery references a table in schema public<br>    Then every link to an ACE record is a varchar(255) business key with no foreign key: account_key, person_key, asset_key, payment_code and contact_key<br>    Then the conformance test reports 1,062 of the 1,062 table fields matched by name and physical type<br>    Then the 30 recovery.v_ace_* views ship separately in RWB-E1-069 and add their 304 fields to the dictionary<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Dictionary published<br>    Given the data dictionary and the data product seed loaded by the migration, and the catalog loaded for DP01<br>    When a developer queries v_data_dictionary for entity "v_ace_account" and field "days_past_due"<br>    Then the row holds field ID "DF-ACE-018", data type "NUMBER", physical type "integer"<br>    Then source system "ACE", source field "public.account.delay_days"<br>    Then data product "DP01", system of record "ACE (ingested from ALS)", refresh cycle "DAILY", owner role "RW-DATASTEW" and the owner user ID of DP01<br>    Then the row for entity "account_ext" and field "dispute_open" holds field ID "DF-EXT-028", data type "BOOLEAN", physical type "boolean", source system "Workbench" and data product "DP01"<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 One system of record<br>    Given a catalog load file with the 14 rows DP01 to DP14, each with one system of record and one owner user ID<br>    When the reference data administrator loads it with valid from 2026-11-30<br>    Then data_product holds 14 rows<br>    Then each row has a definition, exactly one system of record and one owner user ID<br>    Then DP01 names system of record "ACE (ingested from ALS)" and refresh cycle "DAILY"<br>    Then DP03 names system of record "Recovery Workbench" and refresh cycle "ON_CHANGE"<br>    Then DP06 names system of record "ACE (ingested from CCMS, SVAF, AMIS and RPS)", refresh cycle "WEEKLY" and expected by "MON 08:00"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Failed migration leaves no partial schema<br>    Given a copy of the migration in which the statement creating table valuation_ext fails<br>    When the pipeline applies it in DEV<br>    Then the pipeline stage fails and names the failing statement<br>    Then the recovery schema holds none of the tables of that migration<br>    Then the Flyway history has no successful row for version 1.001<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Column without a dictionary entry fails the build<br>    Given a branch that adds the column account_ext.branch_code with no sys_fields row<br>    When the conformance test runs in the pipeline<br>    Then the test fails with "account_ext.branch_code has no data dictionary entry"<br>    Then a sys_fields row DF-EXT-028 whose column is changed to type text fails the test with "DF-EXT-028 physical type boolean expected, text found"<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Repeated migration has no effect<br>    Given the migration version 1.001 already applied in SIT<br>    When the pipeline runs again in SIT<br>    Then Flyway reports version 1.001 as already applied<br>    Then the row counts of sys_objects (122) and sys_fields (1,366) are unchanged<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Catalog row with two systems of record is refused<br>    Given a catalog load file in which DP06 names system of record "ACE (ingested from CCMS); SVAF"<br>    Given DP11 has no owner user ID<br>    When the reference data administrator loads it<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-003 for DP06 and for DP11<br>    Then data_product keeps the rows of the version in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Catalog with a missing product is refused<br>    Given a catalog load file with 13 rows and no row for DP09<br>    When the reference data administrator loads it<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-004 naming "DP09"<br>    Then the system writes no row<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Value outside its list<br>    Given a catalog load file in which row 4 (DP04) names owner role "DP_OWNER" and row 5 (DP05) names refresh cycle "HOURLY"<br>    When the reference data administrator loads it<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-007 "Row 4: owner_role must be one of the role codes in LOV-ROLE." and "Row 5: refresh_cycle must be one of DAILY, WEEKLY, MONTHLY, ON_CHANGE."<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Same catalog file loaded again<br>    Given the catalog version loaded on 2026-11-30 is in force<br>    When the reference data administrator loads the same file again<br>    Then the system writes nothing<br>    Then the response carries MSG-P1-011 "The data product catalog load for 2026-11-30 is already complete or running. It was not run again."<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 Source named on its own is refused<br>    Given a catalog load file in which DP06 names system of record "CCMS"<br>    When the reference data administrator loads it<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-003 "DP06 must name one system of record, written ACE (ingested from X) or Recovery Workbench, or both where the product holds both, and one named owner."<br>    Then no row is written and the version in force stays in use |
| RWB-E1-002 | Case list v1 with case ID, owner and queue | Feature: Case list v1 with case ID, owner and queue<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the business date is 2026-11-30 and v_ace_batch_status shows ALS COMPLETED for 2026-11-30<br>    Given the parameters case.entry.dpd = 90 and recon.caselist.tolerance.pct = 0.00 are in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 List built and reconciled<br>    Given v_ace_account holds 28,140 accounts at 90 days past due or more with data_as_of 2026-11-30<br>    Given P3 excluded 21 of them at entry, so the ACE count to match is 28,119<br>    Given the open cases link 28,119 accounts at 90 days past due or more and 37 accounts below 90 days past due<br>    When the case list step of JOB-04 runs<br>    Then case_list_row holds 28,156 rows for 2026-11-30<br>    Then REC-01 of RWB-E1-011 compares 28,119 with 28,119 and is PASS<br>    Then the run record shows 37 rows below 90 days past due<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Columns present<br>    Given the case list for 2026-11-30 is built<br>    When the extract file for 2026-11-30 is written<br>    Then each row holds case number, account number, product, stage, status, owner, queue, route, total balance and days past due, in that order<br>    Then each row also holds team, business date and refresh time<br>    Then the Power BI data set "Case list v1" offers filters on team, queue and product<br>    Then the row for case "RW-2026-000123" shows account "ACC0000123", product "MORTGAGE", status "ASSIGNED", queue "MTG-BKK-CO", total balance 1,250,000.00 and days past due 96, the values of v_ace_account for that account_key<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Daily refresh<br>    Given the ALS batch for 2026-11-30 completed at 05:40 and JOB-03 completed at 06:52<br>    When the case list step runs and completes at 07:12<br>    Then every row for 2026-11-30 carries refresh time 2026-12-01 07:12<br>    Then the report after the 07:30 data set refresh shows "Refreshed 1 Dec 2026 07:12, data as of 30 Nov 2026"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Last contact from EFS through ACE<br>    Given v_ace_efs_action holds an action "PTP" for an account of case "RW-2026-000123" with contact_at 2026-11-30 15:42<br>    When the case list step runs<br>    Then the row of case "RW-2026-000123" shows last contact 2026-11-30 15:42 and action code "PTP"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 ACE batch not complete<br>    Given v_ace_batch_status shows no COMPLETED ALS row for 2026-11-30 at 08:00<br>    When the DP01 cut-off passes and JOB-04 is triggered<br>    Then the daily completeness gate holds the step and no case_list_row for 2026-11-30 is written<br>    Then the lake keeps the extract file for 2026-11-27<br>    Then the team manager group receives MSG-P1-001 "DP01 Account and arrears is from 2026-11-27. Today's ACE batch has not arrived."<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Allocation not finished<br>    Given JOB-03 for 2026-11-30 has not completed at 07:15<br>    When the case list step starts at 07:15<br>    Then the system builds the list with the owner and queue as they are at 07:15<br>    Then the run record shows check ALLOCATION_COMPLETE NO<br>    Then the report shows "Owners and queues may be incomplete for 30 Nov 2026"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Run repeated for the same date<br>    Given the case list for 2026-11-30 holds 28,156 rows<br>    When the platform administrator restarts the step for 2026-11-30<br>    Then case_list_row still holds 28,156 rows for 2026-11-30 and no case account appears twice<br>    Then the refresh time is the time of the rerun<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Case interface error<br>    Given CaseQueryApi raises an error after 12,000 accounts are read<br>    When the case list step runs<br>    Then the system writes no case_list_row for 2026-11-30<br>    Then the run status is FAILED<br>    Then the rows for 2026-11-27 are unchanged<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Transfer to the lake fails<br>    Given the outbound file adapter fails all its retries for the 2026-11-30 extract file<br>    When the publish step runs with publish_status "RELEASED"<br>    Then the run shows COMPLETED with check PUBLISHED NO<br>    Then the transfer is in the exception queue with a task for the platform administrator<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Case account without an ACE account row<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000456" links account_key "ACC0000456", which has no v_ace_account row for 2026-11-30<br>    When the case list step runs<br>    Then the row for "ACC0000456" has an empty account number, total balance and days past due<br>    Then REC-05 of RWB-E1-011 lists "case_account.account_key ACC0000456" as an orphan for the data steward and the ACE team<br>    Then the case and its case_account link are unchanged<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 Case list access refused<br>    Given user "u-owner-01" holds RW-CASEOWN and not Platform administration R<br>    When the user calls GET /admin/jobs?type=JOB-04<br>    Then the system refuses the call with status 403 and MSG-P4-002 |
| RWB-E1-007 | Integration adapters for outbound files | Feature: Integration adapters for outbound files<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the interface configuration version "IF-2026.11" is in force<br>    Given interface "INT-14" WIN outbound has direction "OUTBOUND", max attempts 3 and retry intervals 5, 15 and 45 minutes<br>    Given interface "INT-14" has owner role "RW-PLATADM"<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 One outbound pattern<br>    Given a developer adds the outbound file "INT-25" debt buyer data tape<br>    When the build pipeline runs<br>    Then the system declares the file as an interface configuration row with direction "OUTBOUND" and uses OutboundFileAdapter<br>    Then the contract test "INT-25" builds the synthetic tape of 120 accounts from debt_sale_account and v_ace_account and passes<br>    Then the architecture test finds no SFTP or file system call outside the package recovery.integration.adapter<br>    Then no interface configuration row has direction "INBOUND"<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Outbound file reads ACE only through the views<br>    Given 85 approved notices for business date 2026-11-10, each with person_key and contact_key<br>    When JOB-10 builds the WIN file "WIN_NOTICE_20261110.txt"<br>    Then each addressee and address is read from v_ace_contact by contact_key<br>    Then no statement of the build reads a public table<br>    Then the outbound item records the ACE business date 2026-11-10 from v_ace_batch_status<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Retry and exception queue<br>    Given the outbound WIN file "WIN_NOTICE_20261110.txt" for 85 notices on 85 cases<br>    Given the SFTP transfer fails on attempts 1, 2 and 3<br>    When the outbox sender runs<br>    Then attempt 2 starts 5 minutes after attempt 1 and attempt 3 starts 15 minutes after attempt 2<br>    Then each case in the file moves to route state "RETRY_QUEUE" after attempt 1 (R-UNI-33)<br>    Then after attempt 3 the item status is "EXCEPTION"<br>    Then each of the 85 cases moves to route state "EXCEPTION_QUEUE" with a task for role "RW-PLATADM" (R-UNI-34)<br>    Then the item shows in the exception queue with message MSG-P2-001<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Outbound file held while the ACE batch is not complete<br>    Given v_ace_batch_status shows the ALS batch for 2026-11-10 with status "FAILED" at 05:20<br>    Given the WIN notice file for 2026-11-10 is due at 16:00<br>    When JOB-10 reaches its build step<br>    Then no file is built and no outbound_item is written<br>    Then the request is held with the reason "DP01 Account and arrears is from 2026-11-07"<br>    Then the platform administrator receives MSG-P1-001 "DP01 Account and arrears is from 2026-11-07. Today's ACE batch has not arrived."<br>    Then the file is built by the next JOB-10 run after EV-26 for 2026-11-10<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Duplicate application event ignored<br>    Given consumer "booking-confirmation" processed event key "EV-10:TRT-0001:v1"<br>    When the same event arrives again with the same key<br>    Then the consumer does not act<br>    Then the hit count for that key becomes 2 (R-UNI-40)<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Two senders pick the same item<br>    Given outbound item "OB-0001" is due<br>    When two sender threads run at 10:00:00<br>    Then the system sends the file once<br>    Then the attempt count is 1<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Resend from the exception queue<br>    Given outbound item "OB-0002" has status "EXCEPTION" after 3 attempts<br>    When a platform administrator resends it<br>    Then the item status is "PENDING" with attempt count 0<br>    Then the system writes the resend to the ACE audit log with the user and the time<br>    Then the response carries message MSG-GEN-005 |
| RWB-E1-008 | Decision log store v1, append only and hash chained | Feature: Decision log store v1, append only and hash chained<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the environment is DEV with synthetic data<br>    Given the decision log store is empty<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Append only<br>    Given an entry with chain_seq 1 and final call "OWNER_ASSIGNED" is stored for case "RW-2026-000123"<br>    When an UPDATE setting final_call to "CASE_TRANSFERRED" and a DELETE of the entry are attempted through the ACE service's database role and again through the schema owner role<br>    Then the system refuses all four statements : by the missing grant for the service role, and with "decision_log_entry is append only" from tr_declog_append_only for the schema owner<br>    Then the entry is unchanged<br>    Then the system writes the refused attempts to the ACE audit log with the table, the operation and the entry ID<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Hash chain<br>    Given 100 entries are appended<br>    When the chain verifier runs from chain_seq 1 to 100<br>    Then every entry_hash equals the SHA-256 of the entry's canonical form<br>    Then every prev_hash equals the entry_hash of the entry before it, and the first prev_hash is 64 zeros<br>    Then the result holds count 100, last chain_seq 100 and that entry's entry_hash<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Seven field groups<br>    Given an entry is appended for decision "D6" on case "RW-2026-000123" with rule "R-UNI-14", rule version "UNI-2026.11", inputs reference "inputs/2026-12-03/7f3a", recommendation, approver "U-6101", approval level "L6", final call "APPROVED", actions and outcome<br>    When it is read with GET /decision-log/{entryId}<br>    Then the response holds inputsRef, ruleId and ruleVersion, recommendation, approverUserId and approvalLevel, finalCall, actions and outcome<br>    Then decidedAt, prevHash and entryHash are present<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Hash chain verified daily<br>    Given the store holds 10,000 entries<br>    Given the actions of the entry with chain_seq 7,412 were changed in the DEV database with the trigger disabled<br>    When the chain verifier runs from chain_seq 1 to 10,000<br>    Then the result names chain_seq 7,412 and its entry ID as the first break<br>    Then the system raises an incident and MSG-P7-008 is sent to the Compliance group within 15 minutes of the run start<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Two appends at the same moment form one chain<br>    Given entries up to chain_seq 50 are stored<br>    When two writers append one entry each in parallel transactions<br>    Then the entries get chain_seq 51 and 52<br>    Then the prev_hash of 52 equals the entry_hash of 51, and the prev_hash of 51 equals the entry_hash of 50<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 A correction is a new entry<br>    Given entry "E-1" records owner "U-6102" by mistake<br>    When a correction entry is appended with corrects_entry_id "E-1" and the right owner "U-6103"<br>    Then entry "E-1" is unchanged<br>    Then GET /decision-log for case "RW-2026-000123" returns both entries in chain order with the correction naming "E-1"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 A failed append leaves nothing behind<br>    Given the database connection for the store is closed in the test double<br>    When a writer appends an entry inside a transaction that also changes the case owner<br>    Then the system raises decisionLogUnavailableException<br>    Then the system stores no entry , the case owner is unchanged and chain_seq is not used<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Reading an entry needs the Decision log right<br>    Given test user "U-6104" holds only RW-PHONECOL, whose Decision log letter is "-"<br>    When the user calls GET /decision-log/{entryId}<br>    Then the system refuses the call with status 403 and message MSG-P4-002 |
| RWB-E1-011 | Reconciliation and data quality report | Feature: Reconciliation and data quality report<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the business date is 2026-11-30 and v_ace_batch_status shows ALS COMPLETED for 2026-11-30<br>    Given the parameter recon.tolerance.pct = 0.00 is in force for REC-01 and REC-02<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Variance under limit<br>    Given the v_ace_account accounts at 90 days past due or more less the exclusions are MORTGAGE 12,256, HIRE_PURCHASE 12,960 and SSME 2,903<br>    Given the case list rows at 90 days past due or more are MORTGAGE 12,256, HIRE_PURCHASE 12,960 and SSME 2,903, with equal balance sums<br>    Given no restructure plan is overdue in v_ace_plan, no payment of the date is unmatched, and every business key resolves to its view<br>    When the reconciliation step of JOB-04 runs<br>    Then the system writes fifteen reconciliation_result rows, REC-01 to REC-05 for each of the three product groups, each with variance 0 and result PASS<br>    Then the system sets the publish status of the case list for 2026-11-30 to RELEASED<br>    Then the report after the 07:30 refresh shows all three products green<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Variance over limit<br>    Given v_ace_account holds 2,903 SSME accounts at 90 days past due or more less the exclusions<br>    Given the case list holds 2,891 SSME rows at 90 days past due or more, a variance of 12 accounts, 0.41 percent<br>    When the reconciliation step runs<br>    Then the REC-01 result for SSME is BREAK with variance 12 and variance percent 0.41<br>    Then the 12 account keys are on the break list for the data steward, with their days past due from v_ace_account<br>    Then the system sets the case list publish for 2026-11-30 to HELD and the lake keeps the case list of 2026-11-27<br>    Then the data steward, platform administrator and team manager groups receive MSG-P1-012 "The case list for 2026-11-30 does not reconcile to ACE for SSME: a difference of 12 accounts, 0.41 percent."<br>    Then the report shows SSME red with the 12 break rows, and MORTGAGE and HIRE_PURCHASE green<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Variance equal to the tolerance<br>    Given the parameter recon.tolerance.pct for REC-01 is changed by a reference load to 0.10 from 2026-11-30<br>    Given the MORTGAGE variance is 0.10 percent and the SSME variance is 0.11 percent<br>    When the reconciliation step runs<br>    Then the MORTGAGE result is PASS and the SSME result is BREAK<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 ACE batch not complete<br>    Given v_ace_batch_status shows no COMPLETED ALS row for 2026-11-30 at 08:00<br>    When the DP01 cut-off passes and JOB-04 is triggered<br>    Then the daily completeness gate holds JOB-04 and the system writes no reconciliation_result for 2026-11-30<br>    Then the case list publish for 2026-11-30 stays HELD<br>    Then the report shows every product as not run with the reason "DP01 is from 2026-11-27"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Rerun after a fix<br>    Given the SSME result for 2026-11-30 is BREAK and the publish is held<br>    Given P3 has created the 12 missing cases and the case list step has been rerun<br>    When the platform administrator restarts the reconciliation step for 2026-11-30<br>    Then the system replaces the fifteen results for 2026-11-30 and all are PASS<br>    Then the system sets the publish status of the date to RELEASED and the case list extract is sent to the lake<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Two runs for the same date<br>    Given two JOB-04 runs for 2026-11-30 start on two nodes<br>    When both reach the reconciliation step<br>    Then reconciliation_result holds one row per check and product group, fifteen rows in all<br>    Then no row is written twice for the same rec_code, business_date and product_group<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Business key without an ACE row<br>    Given case_party on case "RW-2026-000140" carries person_key "P0099881", which has no row in v_ace_customer<br>    When the Given the last case number issued in 2026 is RW-2026-999999<br>    When account A-5010 qualifies for a new case on 2026-12-30<br>    Then the system creates no case for A-5010<br>    Then the job record carries MSG-P3-025 with year 2026<br>    Then the system raises an incident to the platform administrator<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 Two products give two cases<br>    Given v_ace_account holds mortgage account A-5011 and hire purchase account A-5012 for borrower P-0103, both at days_past_due 91<br>    When the entry rule runs<br>    Then two cases exist for P-0103, one with product group "MORTGAGE" and one with "HIRE_PURCHASE"<br><br>  Scenario: AC13 A key with no view row is listed, never invented<br>    Given case RW-2026-000126 links account_key A-5013 and person_key P-0105<br>    Given v_ace_account has no row for A-5013 on 2026-12-01 and v_ace_customer has no row for P-0105<br>    When JOB-02 runs for 2026-12-01 and the intake reads the case links<br>    Then the system creates no case and no party link from a missing view row<br>    Then case RW-2026-000126 keeps its existing links unchanged<br>    Then REC-05 lists account_key A-5013 and person_key P-0105 for the data steward and the ACE team<br>    Then both keys are read again on the next complete ALS batch<br><br>  Scenario: AC14 A role without Case record R is refused<br>    Given user "U-5001" holds RW-REFADM, which has no Case record right<br>    When "U-5001" calls GET /cases/{caseId} for case RW-2026-000123<br>    Then the system refuses the call with status 403 and MSG-P4-002<br>    Then the system writes one AC-DENY event with area "CASE_RECORD" and right "R" |

force with table version 1<br>    Then each queue holds its team code and the role that works it, for example "LIT-BKK-OFF" with "LIT-BKK" and RW-LITOFF<br>    Then 48 member rows are in force<br>    Then the reference version holds the author, the approver and the approval time<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Rollback<br>    Given QUEUE version 3 is in force with an error in queue "DIL-BKK-CO"<br>    Given QUEUE version 2 was in force before it<br>    When rollback is called once for version 3<br>    Then QUEUE version 2 is in force for every queue row<br>    Then each queue keeps its record ID, so cases in the queue keep their queue<br>    Then the system records the rollback with the caller, the time and the reason<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Capacity<br>    Given member U-11 of queue "TDR-BKK-CO" has capacity 40 and holds 40 open cases<br>    When a 41st case is offered for U-11<br>    Then the capacity service answers "AT_CAP" for U-11<br>    Then names the overflow queue "TDR-BKK-OVF" for the case<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Unknown team code<br>    Given the QUEUE sheet has queue "XYZ-CO" on row 7 with team code "XYZ"<br>    When it is loaded<br>    Then the system "NPA-BKK-CO" has capacity cap 0<br>    When the QUEUE sheet is loaded<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-GEN-002 for capacity cap with minimum 1 and maximum 500<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Author cannot approve<br>    Given QUEUE version 4 was loaded by user U-90<br>    When U-90 approves it<br>    Then the system refuses the approval with MSG-P7-002<br>    Then version 4 stays VALIDATED and is not in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Rollback with no earlier version<br>    Given QUEUE version 1 is the only version<br>    When rollback is called for version 1<br>    Then the system refuses the call with MSG-P3-015<br>    Then version 1 stays in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 The same file loaded twice<br>    Given the QUEUE sheet for valid from 2026-12-15 was loaded as version 5<br>    When the same file is loaded again for valid from 2026-12-15<br>    Then the response returns version 5<br>    Then the system writes no new version<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 A version from version 2<br>    Then on 2026-12-15 it answers from version 3<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Member without the queue role<br>    Given member U-12 on row 22 of the QUEUE_MEMBER sheet is listed on queue Scenario: AC11 Overflow loop<br>    Given queue "DS-BKK-CO" overflows to "DS-BKK-OVF" and "DS-BKK-OVF" overflows to "DS-BKK-CO"<br>    When the QUEUE sheet is loaded<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-P1-002 and check "overflow queues form a loop"<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 Required column empty<br>    Given queue row 3 has no assignment method<br>    When the QUEUE sheet is loaded<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-GEN-001 for assignment method on row 3 |

queue "MTG-BKK-CO" has the overflow queue "MTG-BKK-OVF"<br>    Given the business date is 2026-12-01<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 A new case is placed in the queue its row names<br>    Given a new case RW-2026-000201 with status "NEW", product group "MORTGAGE", region "BKK" and exposure band "B2"<br>    Given allocation row "ALLOC-014" matches product group "MORTGAGE", region "BKK" and any exposure band, with target queue "MTG-BKK-CO"<br>    When the morning allocation run starts<br>    Then the system places the case in queue "MTG-BKK-CO"<br>    Then the case team is "MTG-BKK" and the region is "BKK"<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 The owner is chosen by the queue's method<br>    Given queue "MTG-BKK-CO" holds three case owners U-01, U-02 and U-03 with 120, 95 and 140 open cases<br>    Given the last owner given a case in the queue was U-02<br>    When case RW-2026-000201 is placed in the queue<br>    Then the system gives the case to U-03<br>    Then the case status becomes "ASSIGNED"<br>    Then the system writes an assignment record with reason "ALLOCATION" and rule ID "ALLOC-014"<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 An owner at the caseload cap is skipped<br>    Given the last owner given a case was U-02<br>    Given U-03, the next owner in turn, holds 150 open cases<br>    Given U-01 holds 120 open cases<br>    When case RW-2026-000202 is placed in the queue<br>    Then the system gives the case to U-01, the following owner below the cap<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Every owner is at the cap<br>    Given U-01, U-02 and U-03 each hold 150 open cases<br>    When case RW-2026-000203 is placed in queue "MTG-BKK-CO"<br>    Then the system places the case in the overflow queue "MTG-BKK-OVF"<br>    Then the case status stays "NEW" and no owner is set<br>    Then the team manager of "MTG-BKK" receives the allocation summary with MSG-P3-011 and count 1<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 No allocation row matches<br>    Given a new case RW-2026-000204 with product group "SSME" and region "NE3" that matches no allocation row<br>    When the morning allocation run starts<br>    Then the system places the case in the exception queue "RWB-EXC"<br>    Then the case status stays "NEW"<br>    Then the system writes a decision log entry with final call "RULE_GAP", product group "SSME" and region "NE3"<br>    Then the system publishes caseAllocationFailed with reason "NO_ROW_MATCHED"<br>    Then the allocation summary carries MSG-P3-012 with count 1<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Every allocation is logged<br>    Given case RW-2026-000205 matches row "ALLOC-014" and owner U-02 is below the cap<br>    When the case is placed in the queue and given an owner<br>    Then the system writes one decision log entry with rule ID "ALLOC-014", rule version "ALLOC-2026.11", queue "MTG-BKK-CO", owner U-02 and the inputs used<br>    Then if the decision log write fails, the allocation is not made, the case stays "NEW" with no queue and the job record carries MSG-P3-013 for the case<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 A case with an open deviation is not allocated to a case owner<br>    Given a new case RW-2026-000206 with deviation_open = true<br>    When the morning allocation run starts<br>    Then the system places the case in the deviation handler queue "DEV-HANDLER" named by rule R-DEV-01<br>    Then the system sets no case owner and the status stays "NEW"<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 A repeated run for the same date has no further effect<br>    Given the morning allocation run for 2026-12-01 has assigned case RW-2026-000201 to U-03<br>    When the run for 2026-12-01 is started again from the job monitor<br>    Then case RW-2026-000201 keeps owner U-03 and its one assignment record<br>    Then the system writes no second decision log entry for the case<br>    Then the job record shows 1 case skipped as already allocated<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 A run with no new cases<br>    Given no case has status "NEW" on 2026-12-01<br>    When the morning allocation run starts<br>    Then the run completes with 0 cases read<br>    Then the allocation summary for each team shows 0 in overflow and 0 in the exception queue<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 The run waits for case creation<br>    Given JOB-02 has not completed for 2026-12-01 because v_ace_batch_status holds no COMPLETED ALS row for that date<br>    When 07:00 passes<br>    Then JOB-03 has not started for 2026-12-01<br>    Then the job monitor shows JOB-03 waiting on JOB-02 with MSG-P1-001<br>    Then every NEW case keeps no queue and no owner<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 Two runs at the same time allocate each case once<br>    Given a first run is allocating case RW-2026-000207<br>    When a rerun for 2026-12-01 reaches the same case at the same time<br>    Then the rerun skips the locked case<br>    Then the case has exactly one assignment record and one decision log entry for 2026-12-01<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 An owner one case below the cap gets the case<br>    Given U-03 is next in turn and holds 149 open cases<br>    When case RW-2026-000208 is placed in queue "MTG-BKK-CO"<br>    Then the system gives the case to U-03<br>    Then U-03 now holds 150 open cases<br><br>  Scenario: AC13 A queue with no overflow queue sends the case to the exception queue<br>    Given queue "HP-CEN-CO" has no overflow queue<br>    Given every owner in queue "HP-CEN-CO" holds 150 open cases<br>    When case RW-2026-000209 is placed in queue "HP-CEN-CO"<br>    Then the system places the case in the exception queue "RWB-EXC" with reason "NO_OVERFLOW_QUEUE"<br>    Then the case status stays "NEW" |

 When they call the transfer endpoint for case RW-2026-000302 with reason code "MANUAL", transfer reason "R03" and target owner U-22<br>    Then the case owner is U-22<br>    Then the assignment and  \| TDR-BKK-CO   \|<br>      \| TRF-006 \| ApprovalRequested  \| TDR-BKK-APR  \|<br>      \| TRF-008 \| DocumentStored     \| DIL-BKK-CO   \|<br>      \| TRF-009 \| PaymentMissed      \| TDR-BKK-CO   \|<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Admin API<br>    Given a team manager with Queue and allocation U and a litigation officer with Case record U<br>    When the team manager reassigns case RW-2026-000305 to Then the case went to "ON_HOLD" and back to "IN_PROGRESS"<br>    Then each of the three calls has a decision log entry with its reason<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Manual transfer without a reason<br>    When a team manager calls the transfer endpoint for case RW-2026-000306 with no transfer reason<br>    Then the system refuses the call with 422 and MSG-P3-001<br>    Then the case keeps its queue and Given test user "U-1003" has an Entra ID account and no group whose name starts "RWB-SIT-"<br>    When the user opens the Workbench<br>    Then the access request page shows MSG-P4-001 and no case, the permissions of both<br>    Given test user "U-1005" is in groups "RWB-SIT-RW-LITOFF" and "RWB-SIT-RW-LEGAL"<br>    When the user signs in<br>    Then the session holds roles "RW-LITOFF" and "RW-LEGAL"<br>    Then the Documents letters are "CRUA", the union of "CRU" and "CRUA"<br>    Then no permission comes from any role the user does not hold<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 A conflicting role the user signs in<br>    Then the system refuses the session with message MSG-P4-006 naming "RW-SECADM" and "RW-CASEOWN"<br>    Then the system raises an incident to the security administrator queue<br>    Then the system writes one refusal event with reason "ROLE_CONFLICT"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 A service identity is checked like a person<br>    Given managed identity "SY-RULES" is in "PM-2026.12" takes effect at 10:00 on 1 December 2026 and removes R in Reporting from RW-CASEOWN<br>    When the user calls GET /reports/measures at 10:30 in the same session<br>    Then the system "RW-2026-000123"<br>    When it is stored<br>    Then the system refuses the call with 415 and MSG-P5-001<br>    Then the system writes nothing to the containers or the tables<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 bytes<br>    Then the system refuses the file of 26,214,401 bytes with 413 and MSG-P5-017 showing 25 MB<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 The same file stored twice<br>    Given the court notice from AC1 is stored on case "RW-2026-000123"<br>    When the same file with the same hash and type is stored again on that case<br>    Then the system returns the existing document ID<br>    Then the case still    When two users add a new version at the same time<br>    Then one request returns 200 with doc_version 3<br>    Then the other returns 409 with MSG-GEN-004<br>    Then exactly one row with doc_version 3 exists<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Version on a superseded document<br>    Given the appraisal report version 1 is inactive<br>    When a user adds a new version to version 1<br>    Then the phone collector with scope OWN who does not own case "RW-2026-000123"<br>    When the phone collector requests the court notice from AC1<br>    Then the system refuses the request with 403 and MSG-P4-003<br>    Then no metadata or content is returned |

"ALLOC-2026.11" and transfer rule version "TRF-2026.11" are in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Record per event<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000123" is created by JOB-02 and given owner "U-7101" by row second entry carries rule ID "ALLOC-MTG-BKK-01", rule version "ALLOC-2026.11", actor "SY-CASEWF" and an inputs reference<br>    Then both entries pass the schema check and two DecisionRecorded events are in the outbox<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Schema enforced<br>    Given a record for case "RW-2026-000123" with final call "CASE_TRANSFERRED" and no rule version, model version or agent version<br>    When it is written<br>    Then the system refuses it with message MSG-P7-009 naming "versions"<br>    Then the writer logs the error with the case ID and the missing field<br>    Then the system rolls the transfer in the same transaction back<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 A rule ID with no rule version is refused<br>    Given a record with rule ID "R-UNI-14" and an empty rule version<br>    When it is written<br>    Then the system refuses it with message MSG-P7-009 naming "ruleVersion"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 A failed write stops the action<br>    Given the decision log store is unavailable<br>    When JOB-03 gives case "RW-2026-000124" to owner "U-7102"<br>    Then the write raises DecisionLogUnavailableException<br>    Then the case owner stays empty and the case status stays "New"<br>    Then the system raises an incident to the platform administrator queue<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 A repeated event gives one entry<br>    Given event "EV-7f3a-0001" for document "DOC-2026-0009981" attached to case "RW-2026-000123" was written<br>    When the same event with idempotency key "EV-7f3a-0001" arrives again<br>    Then one entry exists for the event<br>    Then the writer returns the first entry's ID and adds 1 to the repeat count for R-UNI-40<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 A write outside a transaction is refused<br>    Given a caller without an open transaction<br>    When it calls the writer<br>    Then the system refuses the call with IllegalTransactionStateException and nothing is written<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 An unknown decision code is refused<br>    Given a record with decision code "D99"<br>    When it is written<br>    Then the system refuses it with message MSG-P7-009 naming "decisionCode" |

 Then the prescription date is 2028-03-15<br>    Then a PRESCRIPTION timer expires on 2028-03-15 with warn_at 2027-09-17<br>    Then GET /cases/{caseId} shows prescription date 2028-03-15<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 No service level for the queue and status<br>    Given no SERVICE_LEVEL version is in force on 2026-12-01<br>    When case RW-2026-000403 is placed in queue "NPA-BKK-CO" with status "ASSIGNED" on 2026-12-01<br>    Then no SERVICE_LEVEL timer starts<br>    Then the system lists the case on the exception queue with MSG-P3-026 for "service level" and "HIRE_PURCHASE ASSIGNED"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 No legal period for the product<br>    Given the LEGAL_PERIOD table has no row for "SSME"<br>    When SSME case RW-2026-000404 is created<br>    Then the prescription date is empty<br>    Then the system lists the case on the exception queue with MSG-P3-026 for "legal period" and "SSME"<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 No payment ever made<br>    Given a hire purchase case RW-2026-000405 with an account that has no last payment date and contract date 2025-06-30<br>    When the case is created<br>    Then the prescription date is 2027-06-30<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Missed timer check runs<br>    Given JOB-05 did not run from 10:00 to 10:40 and three timers passed expires_at in that time<br>    When JOB-05 runs at 10:45<br>    Then the system raises three breaches , one for each timer<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 A breach is raised once<br>    Given the SERVICE_LEVEL timer of case RW-2026-000406 is EXPIRED with escalation level 1<br>    When JOB-05 runs again<br>    Then the system publishes no second TimerBreached for the timer<br>    Then escalation level stays 1<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Decision log unavailable at a breach<br>    Given the decision log store is unavailable<br>    When the SERVICE_LEVEL timer of case RW-2026-000407 passes expires_at<br>    Then the timer stays RUNNING with escalation level 0<br>    Then the job record carries MSG-P7-003 for the timer<br>    Then the system raises the breach by the first run after the store is back<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 A deviation pauses the stage clock and not the prescription clock<br>    Given case RW-2026-000408 2026-12-02 to 2026-12-04 and its expires_at moved by 2 working days<br>    Then the PRESCRIPTION timer stayed RUNNING with the same expires_at<br>    Then the case record shows MSG-P3-004 with the the clear identity number for any role<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 A read of a personal field is audited<br>    Given test user "U-2109" holds RW-CASEOWN, which may see DF-ACE-078 in clear<br>    When holds no field value<br><br>  Scenario: AC13 Only the table's approver role may approve<br>    Given load "PM-2026.12" is "Loaded"<br>    When test user "U-2106" holding RW-COE calls approve on that 50,000,000.00 with no loss limit<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Lookup<br>    Given "DOA-2026.11" is in force<br>    When the router asks who may approve decision D6.4, item 2.1.1, product MORTGAGE, principal 2,400,000.00, loss 20.00 percent and loss amount 480,000.00 on 15 December 2026<br>    Then the lookup returns level L6, the row ID of item 2.1.1 at L6 and version "DOA-2026.11"<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Change<br>    Given "DOA-2026.11" is in force<br>    Given file "DOA-2027.01" adds a row for item 2.1.5 at level L5 with principal up to 10,000,000.00, validFrom "2027-01-01"<br>    When "U-3101" loads it and "U-3102" approves it<br>    Then the rows of "DOA-2026.11" stay readable with valid_to "2026-12-31"<br>    Then a lookup for D6.6, item 2.1.5, MORTGAGE, principal 8,000,000.00 dated 2 January 2027 returns L5 and version "DOA-2027.01"<br>    Then one decision log entry records the approval of "DOA-2027.01" with its valid from date<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 A file with an error is When "U-3101" loads it<br>    Then the system refuses the load with message MSG-P1-002 naming the falling limit and the unknown decision code<br>    Then the system stores no row of "DOA-2026.12" and "DOA-2026.11" stays in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 The loader cannot approve and only Risk may<br>    Given load "DOA-2027.01" was loaded by "U-3101"<br>    When "U-3101" calls approve on it<br> Then the call returns status 503 with message MSG-P7-003 and the load stays "Loaded"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 No limit covers the request<br>    Given "DOA-2026.11" holds no row for product HIRE_PURCHASE<br>    When the router asks who may approve decision D6.4, item 2.1.1, for HIRE_PURCHASE, principal 900,000.00<br>    Then the lookup returns "NOT_COVERED"<br>    Then the system raises an incident to the RW-RISK queue with message MSG-P7-010 naming D6.4 and HIRE_PURCHASE<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 The version valid on the decision date is used<br>    Given "DOA-2026.11" is valid to 31 December 2026 and "DOA-2027.01" from 1 January 2027<br>    When the router looks up D6.6, item 2.1.5, MORTGAGE, principal 8,000,000.00 dated 31 December 2026 and again dated 1 January 2027<br>    Then  Then the banner shows MSG-P4-004 with time "14:30"<br>    Then every entry written for "U-4102" until 14:30 carries the grant ID and incident "INC0004521"<br>    Then the system allows at 14:20 PATCH /tasks/{taskId} on case "RW-2026-000123" by "U-4102" and marked with the grant<br>    Then the system refuses at 14:31 the same call with MSG-P4-002, because RW-PLATADM holds only "R" in Case record, with no action by anyone<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Export logged<br>    Given test user "U-4103" holding RW-TEAMMGR filters the case list by status "ASSIGNED" and product "MORTGAGE" and gets 37 rows<br>    When the user exports the list<br>    Then the system writes one decision log entry with decision code "AC-EXPT", actor "U-4103", the filter, row count 37 and no case ID<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Document read logged<br>    Given test user "U-4101" holding RW-CASEOWN opens document "DOC-2026-0009981" on case "RW-2026-000123"<br>    When GET /documents/{documentId}/content returns the file<br>    Then one entry with decision code "AC-READ", the case ID and the document ID is written before the file is returned<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Break-glass without an IAM grant is refused<br>    Given status 403 and message MSG-P4-007<br>    Then one entry with decision code "AC-DENY" and reason "NO_BREAKGLASS_GRANT" is written<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Break-glass with a missing incident or a short reason is refused<br>    Given test user "U-4102" is in group "RWB-SIT-BREAKGLASS"<br>    When the user calls POST /admin/break-glass with no incident reference<br>    Then the system refuses the call with status 422 and message MSG-GEN-001 for field "incidentReference"<br>    Then when the user calls it with incident "INC0004521" and a reason of 12 characters<br>    Then the system refuses the call with status 422 and message MSG-GEN-002 for field "reason" with min 20 and max 1000<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 A read that cannot be logged returns nothing<br>    Given the decision log store is unavailable<br>    When test user "U-4101" opens case "RW-2026-000123"<br>    Then the call returns status 503 with message MSG-P7-003 and no case data<br>    Then the system raises an incident to the platform administrator queue<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Access audit query<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000123" was read by "U-4101" at 10:15, by "U-4102" at 11:02 under break-glass, and by "U-4101" at 16:40 on 3 December 2026<br>    When test user "U-4105" holding RW-AUDIT calls GET /decision-log with that case ID, decision "AC-READ", from "2026-12-03" and to "2026-12-03"<br>    Then three entries return in time order with actor, time and fields shown<br>    Then the 11:02 entry carries incident "INC0004521"<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Refusals are written to the decision log<br>    Given test user "U-4106" holds only RW-PHONECOL<br>    When the user calls GET /admin/roles<br>    Then the system refuses the call with MSG-P4-002<br>    Then one entry with decision code "AC-DENY", area "PLATFORM_ADMINISTRATION", right "R" and no case ID is written<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 A repeated activation does not open a second grant<br>    Given "U-4102" activated break-glass at 10:30 with  Scenario: AC8 The same event twice<br>    Given DocumentStored for one document on case "RW-2026-000123" is delivered twice with the same event ID<br>    When the evaluator handles both<br>    Then with the header "-- Story: RWB-E1-020"<br>    When the deployment runs<br>    Then the migration applies once<br>    Then its version 12 and its checksum are recorded in "flyway_schema_history_recovery"<br>    Then a second deployment run applies nothing and changes no object<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Drift detected<br>    Given a column "note_x" added by hand to table "queue" in SIT<br>    When the migration check runs<br>    Then the pipeline fails with MSG-P3-016 naming "rwb_case.queue.note_x"<br>    Then no deployment to SIT starts<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Schema name as parameter<br>    Given RWB_SCHEMA set to "rwb_case" in DEV and in SIT<br>    When the migration V13__create_timer.sql runs in DEV and in SIT<br>    Then the table "timer" exists in schema "rwb_case" in both environments<br>    Then the file V13__create_timer.sql is byte for byte the same in both runs<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Applied migration edited<br>    Given V12__add_queue_to_case.sql applied in SIT with checksum 104553219<br>    Given the file changed in the repository so its checksum is 88120477<br>    When the service starts in SIT<br>    Then Flyway validation fails with MSG-P3-016 naming version 12<br>    Then the service does not start<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Literal schema name<br>    Given V14__add_index.sql contains the text "CREATE INDEX ix_a ON recovery.queue (team_code)"<br>    When the migration lint runs<br>    Then the build fails with MSG-P3-016 naming V14__add_index.sql line 1<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 One migration per story<br>    Given V15__a.sql and V16__b.sql both carry the header "-- Story: RWB-E1-028"<br>    When the migration lint runs<br>    Then the build fails with MSG-P3-016 naming both files and RWB-E1-028<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Forward only in PROD<br>    Given the PROD configuration<br>    When a pipeline step calls Flyway clean or an undo migration U12__add_queue_to_case.sql is present<br>    Then the system refuses the step with MSG-P3-016<br>    Then no object in PROD changes<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Migration fails part way<br>    Given V17__add_two_columns.sql whose second statement refers to a table that does not exist<br>    When the deployment runs in DEV<br>    Then the system rolls the first statement back<br>    Then the history records version 17 as failed<br>    Then the service does not start and the run shows "failed" on the job monitor<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Two instances start together<br>    Given two service instances in SIT with migration V18__add_column.sql pending<br>    When both start within one second<br>    Then the system applies V18 exactly once<br>    Then both instances start |
| RWB-E1-045 | Multi-product parameters for Mortgage, HP and SSME | Feature: Multi-product parameters for Mortgage, HP and SSME<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the business date is 2026-12-01<br> "AUTO_LEASE" with valid from 2026-12-05<br>    Given PRODUCT_PARAMETER version 3 and SERVICE_LEVEL version 3 add every required row for "AUTO_LEASE" with valid from 2026-12-05<br>    Given all three versions are approved<br>    When the ACE data for 2026-12-05 holds an "AUTO_LEASE" account at 90 days past due<br>    Then the system creates a case with product group "AUTO_LEASE"<br>    Then the system sets its timers from the "AUTO_LEASE" rows<br>    Then no deployment took place between 2026-12-01 and 2026-12-05<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Missing parameter blocked<br>    Given a SERVICE_LEVEL load with valid from 2026-12-10 that has no row for "SSME" and status "WAITING_PARTNER"<br>    When the load validates<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-P3-034 naming "SSME" and "WAITING_PARTNER"<br>    Then SERVICE_LEVEL version 3 stays in force<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Value outside its range<br>    Given the catalog range for NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS is 1 to 365<br>    When a PRODUCT_PARAMETER load sets NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS to 0 for "SSME"<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-GEN-002 naming "NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS", 1 and 365<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Duplicate product row<br>    Given a PRODUCT_PARAMETER load with two NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS rows for "MORTGAGE", 30 and 45<br>    When the load validates<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-P3-027 naming "MORTGAGE" and "NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS"<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Product removed while cases are open<br>    Given 3 open cases with product group "UNSECURED"<br>    When a PRODUCT load with valid from 2026-12-15 leaves out "UNSECURED"<br>    Then the system refuses the load with MSG-P3-027 naming "UNSECURED" and 3 open cases<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 No parameter in force at run time<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000140" with product group "SSME" and no NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS row for "SSME" in force<br>    When the timer engine sets the notice timer on 2026-12-01<br>    Then the system writes no notice timer<br>    Then the system places the case in the exception queue with MSG-P3-034 naming "SSME" and "NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS"<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Value changes while a timer runs<br>    Given the notice timer of "RW-2026-000101" started on 2026-12-01 and expires on 2026-12-31<br>    Given PRODUCT_PARAMETER version 4 with NOTICE_PERIOD_DAYS 45 for "MORTGAGE" takes effect on 2026-12-03<br>    When the timer engine sets a notice timer on Mortgage case "RW-2026-000150" on 2026-12-03<br>    Then the timer of "RW-2026-000101" still expires on 2026-12-31<br>    Then the timer of "RW-2026-000150" expires on 2027-01-17 |

Given version 3 of "DEMAND-MTG" "TH" is Approved with valid from 2026-12-01<br>    When TemplateCatalog is asked on 2026-11-30 and on 2026-12-01<br>    Then it returns version 2 on 2026-11-30<br>    Then it returns version 3 on 2026-12-01 |

OCR_MAX_ATTEMPTS is 3<br>    Given the OCR service stub in SIT is replaced by the OCR service for AC1 and AC2<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Sample processed<br>    Given the 180 synthetic sample documents, 30 of each of the six agreed types, in Thai and English, each as a CLEAN intake item<br>    When JOB-12 Document intake runs<br>    Then each of the 180 items has ocr_status "DONE" or "RESCAN" and an ocr_ref to its JSON output<br>    Then each page has a document_text row with the page text and its quality<br>    Then the text of the sample pages holds Thai and English words, and the languages recorded per item are "th" and "en" where both appear<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Throughput<br>    Given 500 pages in 125 CLEAN intake items in SIT<br>    When they are processed by JOB-12 with 4 workers<br>    Then all 125 items reach "DONE" or "RESCAN" within 60 minutes of the first item starting<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Unreadable page on a known case<br>    Given an uploaded court notice of 4 pages on case "RW-2026-000123" owned by user "TEST-CO-01"<br>    Given page 3 has quality 0.41 and the other pages are above 0.70<br>    When the OCR step runs<br>    Then the intake item has ocr_status "RESCAN" and rescan_pages "3"<br>    Then "TEST-CO-01" receives MSG-P5-018 naming page 3, the document and case "RW-2026-000123"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Unreadable page with no case yet<br>    Given a mailroom scan of 2 pages with no case and page 1 at quality 0.30<br>    When the OCR step runs<br>    Then the intake item has ocr_status "RESCAN", rescan_pages "1" and review_reason "RESCAN"<br>    Then the system sends no notification<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Quality at the threshold<br>    Given a scan of 1 page with quality 0.70<br>    When the OCR step runs<br>    Then the item has ocr_status "DONE" and no rescan pages<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 OCR service unavailable<br>    Given the OCR service stub returns HTTP 503 for every call<br>    When JOB-12 runs twice<br>    Then each pending item has ocr_status "PENDING" and ocr_attempts 2<br>    Then the job monitor shows the OCR step failed in both runs and an alert is raised to the platform administrator<br>    Then when the service answers again the next run sets the items "DONE"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 File that cannot be read<br>    Given a password-protected PDF as a CLEAN intake item<br>    When the OCR step runs<br>    Then the item has ocr_status "FAILED" and review_reason "UNREADABLE_FILE"<br>    Then no document_text row exists for it<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Repeat run<br>    Given 10 intake items already "DONE" with 25 document_text rows<br>    When JOB-12 runs again<br>    Then the OCR service receives no call for those items<br>    Then they still have 25 document_text rows<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Two workers on one item<br>    Given one CLEAN pending item and 4 workers starting at the same time<br>    When the OCR step runs<br>    Then the OCR service receives exactly one call for the item<br>    Then the item has one set of document_text rows |

to be set false on the stored document<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Score at the threshold<br>    Given a scanned intake item whose top score is APPRAISAL_REPORT 0.80<br>    When classification runs<br>    When classification runs for one item<br>    Then the item has review_reason "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE" and no predicted_type<br>    Then the item records that the fallback was used<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 document_hub_parameter<br>    Given a scanned item whose top score is COURT_NOTICE 0.85<br>    When classification runs<br>    Then the item has review_reason "LOW_CONFIDENCE"<br>    Then no code or deployment changed between the two values<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Personal identifiers masked before the model call<br>    Given a scanned page holding the synthetic national ID number "0000000000000" and account number "TEST-ACC-0001"<br>    When classification runs<br>    Then the text received by the classifier stub holds tokens in place of both values |

the decision log store is unavailable<br>    When NoticeApi.generate is called for case "RW-2026-000123", notice type "DEMAND"<br>    Then the call fails with MSG-P7-003<br>    Then no notice row and row for 2026-11-25 and v_ace_account.data_as_of is 2026-11-24<br>    When NoticeApi.generate is called for case "RW-2026-000123", notice type "DEMAND"<br>    Then the system generates the notice from
