Person not on the case<br>    Given person_key "P-0999" is not a party on case "RW-2026-000123"<br>    When NoticeApi.generate is called with person_key "P-0999"<br>    Then the system refuses it with MSG-P4-003<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Proof of delivery read from ACE<br>    Given notice "N-0001" on case "RW-2026-000123" has notice_ref "WIN-77001"<br>    Given v_ace_pod holds pod_key "POD-0001" with notice_ref "WIN-77001", sent_date 2026-11-27, barcode "RR123456789TH" and delivery_status "DELIVERED"<br>    When an officer reads notice "N-0001" through GET /cases/{caseId}/notices<br>    Then the "940" with confidence 0.9900 on item "IN-0003"<br>    When extractStep runs<br>    Then area_sq_wa has state "REVIEW"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Confidence equal to the threshold passes<br>    Given the "IN-0007"<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 A repeated run has no effect<br>    Given item "IN-0001" has extract_status "DONE"<br>    When extractStep runs again<br>    Then the system does not read "IN-0001" stores no second document and "IN-0109" is linked to "DOC-0109"<br>    Then the screen shows MSG-P5-024 naming "DOC-0109"<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 Discard an unreadable item<br>    Given intake item 2026."<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Proof of delivery<br>    Given notice "N-0001" is "Sent" with barcode "RR123456789TH"<br>    When v_ace_pod holds a row for 2026-11-30 with barcode "RR123456789TH" and delivered date 2026-11-28<br>    Then case "RC-2026-000123" has a running "NOTICE_PERIOD" timer started on 2026-11-28<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Interface contract agreed with WIN<br>    Given the sample INT-15 file "WIN-POD-SAMPLE-v1" of 25 records supplied by the WIN owner<br>    When ACE ingests it and the Workbench reads v_ace_pod<br>    Then all 25 rows are present in v_ace_pod with 0 refused<br>    Then v_ace_batch_status holds source "WIN" with feed_version "WIN-POD-SAMPLE-v1" for the business date<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 File not acknowledged<br>    Given the WIN file of 2026-11-25 holding "N-0001" is not acknowledged<br>    When JOB-10 runs on 2026-11-26, 2026-11-27 and 2026-11-28 and none is acknowledged<br>    Then the system sends "N-0001" in each of the three files and stays "Generated"<br>    Then after the third failure a task is raised to the litigation officer team with MSG-GEN-006 naming "WIN"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 The WIN batch fails its check in ACE<br>    Given the ACE batch for source "WIN" of 2026-11-30 has status "FAILED" at its record count check<br>    When the proof of delivery for 2026-11-30 is read<br>    Then the system reads no v_ace_pod row for that date and no notice changes state<br>    Then the notice data product shows MSG-P1-001 naming "DP07 Legal" and 2026-11-23<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Unknown reference held<br>    Given v_ace_pod holds 30 rows for 2026-11-30 and 1 row has barcode "RR000000000TH" and notice_ref "WIN-99999" matching no notice<br>    When the proof of delivery is read<br>    Then 29 notices update their delivery fields<br>    Then the system holds 1 row on the exception list with check "References"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 The same proof of delivery read twice<br>    Given the v_ace_pod rows for business date 2026-11-30 have been read<br>    When the same rows are read again<br>    Then one delivery is recorded per notice and the repeat is counted under rule R-UNI-40<br>    Then no notice changes state a second time<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Repeated dispatch run<br>    Given JOB-10 has written notice "N-0001" in the file of 2026-11-25<br>    When JOB-10 is rerun on 2026-11-25 from the job monitor<br>    Then the second file does not hold "N-0001"<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Barcode missing after three days<br>    Given notice "N-0002" was in the file of 2026-11-25 and no barcode has returned<br>    When the daily reconciliation REC-05 runs on 2026-11-28<br>    Then the system lists "N-0002" as a break for the platform administrator on SCR-45<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Repeated delivery record<br>    Given notice "N-0001" is "Delivered" with callback date 2026-11-28<br>    When v_ace_pod holds a second row with barcode "RR123456789TH" and callback_date 2026-11-28<br>    Then the notice is unchanged<br>    Then the system writes no second NoticeDelivered<br>    Then the system counts the repeat under rule R-UNI-40<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 Proof of delivery batch late<br>    Given the WIN batch due on 2026-11-30 is not COMPLETE on 2026-12-01<br>    When a litigation officer opens case "RC-2026-000123" on 2026-12-01<br>    Then the notice section shows MSG-P1-001 naming "DP07 Legal" and 2026-11-23 |
| RWB-E1-056 | Run books and monitoring for every Epic 1 Workbench job | Feature: Run books and monitoring for every Epic 1 Workbench job<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given job_catalog holds 16 rows with first release "E1": JOB-02, JOB-03, JOB-04, JOB-05, JOB-06, JOB-07, JOB-08, JOB-10, JOB-11, JOB-12, JOB-13, JOB-14, JOB-15, JOB-17, AGT-01 and CHK-01<br>    Given the daily case list chain is JOB-02, then JOB-03, JOB-04, JOB-07 and JOB-08, with JOB-05 every five minutes<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Run book present<br>    Given the 16 rows in job_catalog<br>    When RunbookCompletenessTest runs in the pipeline<br>    Then each of the 16 jobs has a file in docs/operations/runbooks<br>    Then each file has the sections purpose, schedule, owner, dependencies, failure signs, checks, restart steps and escalation<br>    Then every run book of a job that reads ACE names v_ace_batch_status in Dependencies and the stale marker and EV-27 AceDataLate in Failure signs<br>    Then the run book for CHK-01 names the view conformance check and the dictionary load through RWB-E1-043 in Checks and Restart steps<br>    Then no file names an inbound load job, a file arrival, a load rerun, a data quality exception console or a table in schema public<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Restart by run book<br>    Given JOB-02 failed at 05:40 on business date 2026-11-30 at its case creation step, because v_ace_account returned no row for an account at 90 days past due<br>    When a platform administrator follows the restart steps, confirms in v_ace_batch_status that ALS is COMPLETED for 2026-11-30, and calls POST /admin/jobs/{jobId}/rerun at 06:05<br>    Then JOB-02 restarts from its failed step and ends with status "COMPLETED" at 06:20<br>    Then JOB-03 runs after JOB-02 and the case list shows the cases with data as of 2026-11-30<br>    Then the run records no developer action<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Monitoring in place<br>    Given every job in job_catalog ran at least once in SIT<br>    When the platform administrator opens the workbook "Recovery jobs"<br>    Then each of the 16 jobs shows its last run time, status and duration<br>    Then the workbook shows the last complete ACE business date per source from v_ace_batch_status beside the jobs<br>    Then GET /admin/jobs returns the same last run, status, startedAt and endedAt for each job<br>    Then GET /admin/scheduled-jobs returns 16 entries with owner role, schedule, run book path and alert threshold<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Missing run book blocks the build<br>    Given a developer adds a Quartz job "JOB-20" with a run book that has no "Escalation" section<br>    When the pipeline runs<br>    Then the build fails with message MSG-P2-005 naming job "JOB-20" and section "Escalation"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Rerun while running refused<br>    Given JOB-04 started at 06:40 and is running<br>    When a platform administrator calls POST /admin/jobs/{jobId}/rerun for JOB-04 at 06:45<br>    Then the response is 409 with message MSG-P2-006<br>    Then JOB-04 continues its run<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Job running past its threshold<br>    Given JOB-02 has an alert threshold of 45 minutes (assumed; DL11)<br>    When JOB-02 has run for 46 minutes on business date 2026-12-01<br>    Then the data steward group and the platform administrator group receive message MSG-P2-004<br>    Then the system does not stop JOB-02<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Rerun of a completed job changes nothing<br>    Given JOB-04 completed for business date 2026-12-01<br>    When a platform administrator reruns it for the same date<br>    Then the response is 409 with message MSG-GEN-004<br>    Then case_list_row still holds one row per case account for 2026-12-01<br>    Then reconciliation_result still holds one row per product group for 2026-12-01<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 A late ACE batch holds the job<br>    Given v_ace_batch_status shows EFS for business date 2026-12-01 with status "FAILED" at 05:20<br>    When the freshness step runs<br>    Then data_product_load marks DP05 Contact and promises "STALE" at 05:20 and EV-27 AceDataLate is published<br>    Then the case list step of JOB-04 does not start for 2026-12-01 and its run is recorded "WAITING"<br>    Then the run book for JOB-04 says to wait for the EFS batch and not to rerun JOB-04<br>    Then when the EFS batch completes at 08:40, the daily gate returns COMPLETE and JOB-04 starts for 2026-12-01<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 The view conformance test has a run book<br>    Given a branch adds column "legacy_bucket" to recovery.v_ace_account with no sys_fields row<br>    When the pipeline runs CHK-01<br>    Then CHK-01 fails with "v_ace_account.legacy_bucket has no data dictionary entry"<br>    Then the merge is blocked<br>    Then the run book for CHK-01 gives the fix: load the dictionary row through RWB-E1-043 and rerun CHK-01 |

job runs<br>    Then the system writes no document_intake row for it<br>    Then the file is in the rejected folder of branch "BR-0123"<br>    Then the job record shows 1 refused on "BR-0123" with MSG-P5-007 and reason MSG-P5-001<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 A file above the size limit is refused<br>    Given file "scan-0003.pdf" of 25.1 MB and file "scan-0004.pdf" of 25.0 MB in the scanner folder of branch "BR-0123"<br>    When the job runs<br>    Then the system refuses "scan-0003.pdf" with reason MSG-P5-017 and moved to the rejected folder<br>    Then "scan-0004.pdf" has a document_intake row<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 An unknown or suspended partner is refused<br>    Given file "notice-0005.pdf" in the SFTP subfolder "LF-099", which is no partner_code<br>    Given file "notice-0006.pdf" in the SFTP subfolder "LF-008", a partner with panel_status "SUSPENDED"<br>    When the job runs<br>    Then the system writes no document_intake row for either file<br>    Then the job record shows MSG-P5-007 with reasons "PARTNER_UNKNOWN" and "PARTNER_NOT_ACTIVE"<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 A duplicate file is not taken in twice<br>    Given file "scan-0001.pdf" with hash "H1" from branch "BR-0123" was taken in on 30 November<br>    When the same file arrives again from branch "BR-0123" on 2 December<br>    Then the system writes no second document_intake row<br>    Then the system moves the file to the processed folder and counted as a duplicate<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 An empty run<br>    Given no new file or email on any channel<br>    When the job runs<br>    Then the job record shows intakeStep complete with 0 items on each channel<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 The intake container does not accept writes<br>    Given the intake container refuses writes<br>    When the job runs with file "scan-0007.pdf" in the folder of branch "BR-0123"<br>    Then the system writes no document_intake row<br>    Then "scan-0007.pdf" stays in the scanner folder<br>    Then on the next run with the container available the file is taken in<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 Two failed runs raise an alert<br>    Given the run at 10:00 failed because the SFTP inbound folder could not be read<br>    When the run at 10:15 fails for the same reason<br>    Then the platform administrator receives MSG-P5-008 naming "PARTNER_SFTP"<br>    Then the run at 10:30 tries again<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 The job runs once across two instances<br>    Given two service instances are running<br>    When the 09:15 run is due<br>    Then the system records one run for 09:15<br>    Then each file has one document_intake row<br><br>  Scenario: AC13 An email with no usable attachment<br>    Given an email to "MBX-RECOVERY-01" with no attachment<br>    Given an email with only a 6 KB signature image<br>    When the job runs<br>    Then the system writes no document_intake row for either email<br>    Then both emails are in the archive folder<br><br>  Scenario: AC14 A file seen again after a failed move<br>    Given file "scan-0008.pdf" was taken in at 11:00 and the move to the processed folder failed<br>    When the run at 11:15 sees "scan-0008.pdf" again<br>    Then the system writes no second document_intake row<br>    Then the system moves the file to the processed folder<br><br>  Scenario: AC15 Outside business hours<br>    Given the time is 18:15 on a working day<br>    When the scheduler checks JOB-12<br>    Then no run starts until 08:00 the next working day<br><br>  Scenario: AC16 An item taken in is scanned before OCR<br>    Given file "scan-0009.pdf" was taken in by intakeStep with an empty scan_result<br>    When ocrStep runs in the same job before scanStep has set scan_result<br>    Then ocrStep does not read "scan-0009.pdf"<br>    Then after scanStep sets scan_result "CLEAN" ocrStep reads it |
| RWB-E1-058 | Agent platform set-up [E1a] | Feature: Agent platform set-up [E1a]<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the test agent "ECHO" has policy version "P1" approved with model "MODEL-A-2026-10", one AgentRunCompleted event to the outbox with the run ID, agent "ECHO", the case ID, outcome "ANSWERED" and fallback false<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Prompt versions<br>    Given prompt version "PR1" is in force for agent "ECHO"<br>    When RW-COE loads prompt version "PR2" and RW-RISK approves it with valid from 2026-11-20<br>    Then prompt version "PR2" exists with status "APPROVED"<br>    Then prompt version "PR1" stays readable through GET /admin/agent-configs with its text hash unchanged<br>    Then a run on 2026-11-19 uses "PR1" and a run on 2026-11-20 uses "PR2"<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Schema check<br>    Given the model stub returns an answer with field "summary" missing from the output schema of "ECHO"<br>    When the guardrail runs<br>    Then the system rejects the output with run status "GUARDRAIL_FAILED" and fallback true<br>    Then the guardrail result holds check code "SCHEMA" with the missing field name<br>    Then the count of check code "SCHEMA" failures for "ECHO" on the business date rises by 1<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Evaluation suite<br>    Given labeled sample "ECHO-S1" version 1 with 20 rows for agent "ECHO"<br>    When RW-COE starts the evaluation of "ECHO" positives, false positives, false negatives, precision and recall<br>    Then each score row holds policy version "P1", prompt version "PR1" and model version "MODEL-A-2026-10"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 Guardrails<br>    Given an agent with the allowed tool list "read_case"<br>    When it calls the tool "propose_deviation", which is not on its list<br>    Then the system refuses the tool call and not executed<br>    Then the agent run holds status "GUARDRAIL_FAILED", check code "TOOL_NOT_ALLOWED" and fallback true<br>    Then the orchestrator returns a fallback result with reason "TOOL_NOT_ALLOWED" so the calling step takes the rule path<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 The model does not answer inside the timeout<br>    Given the model stub does not answer for 61 seconds<br>    When the orchestrator runs agent "ECHO" for case "RW-2026-000102"<br>    Then the system ends the call at 60 seconds<br>    Then the agent run holds status "TIMED_OUT", fallback true and latency 60,000 ms<br>    Then the orchestrator returns a fallback result with reason "TIMED_OUT" (R-UNI-32)<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Personal data is masked before the model call<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000103" has a borrower with a synthetic national ID number and a synthetic mobile number<br>    When the orchestrator builds the request to the model endpoint<br>    Then the request body holds the masking tokens returned by the P4 masking service for SY-AGENT<br>    Then the request body, the agent run row and the application log hold no national ID number, phone number, name or account number<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 A configuration file that fails a check is refused<br>    Given a policy file for agent "ECHO" with timeout_seconds 0<br>    When RW-COE loads it through POST /admin/agent-configs<br>    Then the system refuses the load with 422 and MSG-P10-001 naming check "timeout_seconds between 1 and 120"<br>    Then policy version "P1" stays in force and no new version row exists<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 The loader cannot approve their own version<br>    Given user "coe.user01" loaded policy version "P2" for agent "ECHO"<br>    When user "coe.user01" calls POST /admin/agent-configs/{versionId}/approve for "P2"<br>    Then the system refuses the approval with 409 and MSG-P7-002<br>    Then version "P2" keeps status "LOADED"<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 An answer below the confidence floor falls back<br>    Given the model stub returns a valid answer with confidence 0.79<br>    When the guardrail runs with confidence floor 0.80<br>    Then the agent run holds status "BELOW_FLOOR" and fallback true<br>    Then the orchestrator returns a fallback result with reason "BELOW_FLOOR"<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 A repeated run request has no further effect<br>    Given agent "ECHO" ran for case "RW-2026-000101" with idempotency key "ECHO:RW-2026-000101:2026-11-30"<br>    When the same request arrives again with the same key<br>    Then no second agent run row and no second model call exist<br>    Then the system returns the first result and the idempotency hit is counted (R-UNI-40)<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 Two approvals of one version at the same time<br> OCR_AND_MODEL both have mean F1 0.9310 for "COURT_NOTICE"<br>    When the proposal is built<br>    Then the system proposes OCR_TEMPLATE<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 A sample row for a document that does no v_ace_* view has a column without a row and no row without a column<br>    Then the four extension tables party_ext, contact_point_ext, account_ext and valuation_ext hold 49 fields, DF-EXT-001 to tables<br>    Given a class in "recovery.integration.internal" that uses the entity "recovery.casewf.internal.RecoveryCaseEntity"<br>    When the pipeline runs<br>    Then the build fails with | RWB-TE-004 | Masked data in SIT and UAT | Feature: Masked data in SIT and UAT<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given masking rule version "MASK-2026.11" is in force<br>    Given extract "PII-SIT-20261120" for business date 2026-11-19 has a manifest with masking rule version "MASK-2026.11"<br>    Given the manifest lists 28,119 case accounts: Mortgage 12,256, Hire purchase 12,960 and SSME 2,903, with row counts per product<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Masked copy<br>    Given a masked copy from the PII service<br>    When it is loaded into SIT<br>    Then every column tagged is_personal in interaction and document_field holds a token or a masked value<br>    Then the row counts keep their shape: the count per table and per product equals the manifest<br>    Then each person key joins to the same case_party and case_account rows as in the extract<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 A personal value left in the copy<br>    Given interaction row 4,512 of the extract holds a 13-digit value with a valid national ID check digit in summary<br>    When the copy is loaded into SIT<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-002 and check "unmasked value in interaction.summary"<br>    Then the previous SIT copy stays in use<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Totals that do not match the manifest<br>    Given the case_account file holds 28,118 rows<br>    When the copy is loaded into SIT<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-002 and check "Control totals case_account 28,118 against 28,119"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 A copy aimed at DEV<br>    Given the target environment is DEV<br>    When the masked copy load starts<br>    Then the system refuses it with MSG-P1-002 and check "masked copies load only into SIT and UAT"<br>    Then the system reads no file<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 The same extract twice<br>    Given extract "PII-SIT-20261120" was loaded into SIT on 2026-11-20<br>    When it is loaded into SIT again<br>    Then the system skips the load and the report names the load of 2026-11-20<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 A masking rule version that is not in force<br>    Given a manifest with masking rule version "MASK-2026.10"<br>    When the copy is loaded into UAT<br>    Then the system refuses the load in full with MSG-P1-002 and check "masking rule version MASK-2026.10 is not in force"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 A second load while one runs<br>    Given a masked copy load into SIT is running<br>    When a second load into SIT starts<br>    Then the system refuses the second load with MSG-P2-006 |
| RWB-TE-005 | Rule table test harness | Feature: Rule table test harness<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the Restructure route set version "RES-2026.11" with 37 rows, R-RES-01 to R-RES-37<br> test case<br>    Given version "RES-2026.13" where row "R-RES-12" names test case "RT-RES-012" that is not in the Tests sheet<br>    When the harness runs<br>    Then the system marks the version "FAILED" with MSG-P3-014 naming "R-RES-12" and "RT-RES-012"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 Two rows hit one input<br>    Given version "RES-2026.14" where the conditions of "R-RES-09" and "R-RES-10" both hold for an offer outside the range with deviation_flag "Y"<br>    When the harness runs the test of "R-RES-10"<br>    Then the test fails with MSG-P3-014 naming "R-RES-09" and "R-RES-10"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 No row hits<br>    Given version "RES-2026.15" where the condition of "R-RES-28" was changed so no row holds for a payment inside the cure period<br>    When the harness runs the test of "R-RES-28"<br>    Then the test fails with MSG-P3-014 naming "R-RES-28" as expected and no row hit<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Missing fact<br>    Given the test case of "R-RES-30" gives no value for "break_count"<br>    When the harness runs it<br>    Then the test fails with MSG-P3-014 naming the fact "break_count"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Repeated run<br>    Given the harness ran on "RES-2026.11" with 37 passes<br>    When it runs again on "RES-2026.11"<br>    Then the results are the same 37 passes<br>    Then the system stores one result set for the version<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Build pipeline<br>    Given the rule files in the repository hold the ten route sets with 448 rows<br>    When the build runs<br>    Then the build runs 448 rule tests named by rule ID<br>    Then a failing rule test fails the build |
| RWB-TE-006 | Outbox and retry for outbound work | Feature: Outbox and retry for outbound work<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given consumer "win-dispatch" takes work request WIN_DISPATCH, holds the case step and has owner role RW-PLATADM<br>    Given consumers "ledger" and "reporting-feed" take CaseAllocated, CaseTransferred and CaseStateChanged, do not hold the case step and have owner role RW-PLATADM<br>    Given the retry settings are 3 retries at 1, 5 and 15 minutes after the previous attempt<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000601" has notice "NT-SYN-0601" waiting for approval<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Sent after commit<br>    Given a notice approved<br>    When the transaction commits at 14 December 2026 10:00:00<br>    Then the outbox holds one WIN_DISPATCH request for case "RW-2026-000601" with one delivery for "win-dispatch"<br>    Then the header holds the event ID, idempotency key "WIN_DISPATCH:NT-SYN-0601", schema version 1, occurred time and actor<br>    Then the payload holds notice and case references only, with no name, address or identifier<br>    Then at the 10:01 run the sender delivers it and "win-dispatch" passes the notice to OutboundFiles.enqueue for the day's WIN file<br>    Then the delivery has status DELIVERED<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Retry limit<br>    Given a target down for longer than the retry limit<br>    When "win-dispatch" fails at 10:01, 10:02, 10:07 and 10:22<br>    Then the system makes no fifth attempt<br>    Then the item moves to the exception queue: the "win-dispatch" delivery has status EXCEPTION and attempt count 4<br>    Then the system raises task RESOLVE_OUTBOX_DELIVERY on case "RW-2026-000601" for role RW-PLATADM<br>    Then event RETRY_LIMIT_REACHED moves the case to EXCEPTION_QUEUE by R-UNI-36 with one decision log entry<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Nothing is sent for a change that did not commit<br>    Given the notice approval fails after OutboxWriter.publish and the transaction rolls back<br>    When the sender runs<br>    Then no event and no delivery exist for "NT-SYN-0601"<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 A retry that succeeds<br>    Given "win-dispatch" fails at 10:01 and the case moves to RETRY_QUEUE by R-UNI-33<br>    When the attempt at 10:02 succeeds<br>    Then the system delivers the delivery status with attempt count 2<br>    Then event RETRY_SUCCEEDED returns the case to the state it paused in by R-UNI-35<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 The same event is written once<br>    Given the approval of "NT-SYN-0601" is submitted twice with idempotency key "WIN_DISPATCH:NT-SYN-0601"<br>    When both transactions commit<br>    Then the outbox holds one event with that key<br>    Then the second transaction receives DuplicateOutboxKeyException and changes nothing<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Two sender instances<br>    Given 200 due deliveries and two service instances<br>    When JOB-11 runs on both at 10:01<br>    Then the system makes each delivery once<br>    Then the delivery log holds 200 calls<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Order per case and consumer<br>    Given event CaseTransferred with case sequence 11 and event CaseStateChanged with case sequence 12 for case "RW-2026-000601"<br>    Given the "ledger" consumer fails on sequence 11<br>    When the sender runs<br>    Then the system does not deliver sequence 12 to "ledger" until sequence 11 is DELIVERED<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Consumers are independent<br>    Given event CaseAllocated for case "RW-2026-000601"<br>    When "ledger" fails and "reporting-feed" succeeds at 10:01<br>    Then the system delivers the "reporting-feed" delivery and the "ledger" delivery is RETRYING with next attempt 10:02<br>    Then the case route state does not change, because "ledger" does not hold the case step<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Sending an exception delivery again<br>    Given delivery "OBD-SYN-0001" has status EXCEPTION<br>    When the platform administrator sends it again<br>    Then the response is 200 with message MSG-GEN-005<br>    Then the delivery status is PENDING with attempt count 0 and due at the next run<br>    When the platform administrator sends delivery "OBD-SYN-0002" with status DELIVERED again<br>    Then the response is 409 with message MSG-P2-022<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 An event with no case<br>    Given an event with no case whose only consumer fails 4 times<br>    When the retries are used<br>    Then the delivery status is EXCEPTION<br>    Then the system sends an alert to the platform administrator within 15 minutes<br>    Then no task and no case event are raised<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 Retry settings come from configuration<br>    Given the retry delays are changed in the configuration service to 2, 10 and 30 minutes at 09:00<br>    When "ledger" fails at 10:01<br>    Then the next attempt is due at 10:03 |

dates 2026-12-02, 2026-12-03 and 2026-12-04 while both instances were down<br>    When the service restarts on 2026-12-04 at 11:00<br>    Then JOB-03 runs for 2026-12-02, then 2026-12-03, then 2026-12-04<br>    Then the system logs each run with its business date<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 More missed dates than the limit<br>    Given 7 business dates with passed COLLINS loads and no JOB-03 run<br>    When the service restarts<br>    Then no automatic catch-up run starts<br>    Then an alert with MSG-P3-031 names JOB-03 and the 7 dates<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Dependency not met<br>    Given JOB-02 failed for business date 2026-12-03<br>    When JOB-03 is due for 2026-12-03<br>    Then JOB-03 does not run and its run is recorded "WAITING" with MSG-P3-031 naming "JOB-02"<br>    Then when JOB-02 succeeds for 2026-12-03 at 08:10 JOB-03 runs for 2026-12-03<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Instance stops part way<br>    Given JOB-04 is running for 2026-12-02 on "ACE-1"<br>    When "ACE-1" stops<br>    Then "ACE-2" runs JOB-04 again for 2026-12-02 within the cluster check-in interval of 20 seconds<br>    Then the run log shows one "FAILED" run on "ACE-1" and one "SUCCEEDED" run on "ACE-2"<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 Allocation late<br>    Given JOB-03 for 2026-12-02 has not succeeded at 07:00<br>    When the deadline check runs at 07:00<br>    Then an alert with MSG-P3-031 naming JOB-03, 2026-12-02 and 07:00 reaches the operations channel by 07:15<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Run book for every job<br>    Given the run book folder holds run books for JOB-03 and JOB-05 only<br>    When the build runs<br>    Then the build fails naming JOB-04 |
| RWB-TE-008 | Decision log hash chain check | Feature: Decision log hash chain check<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given the environment is SIT with the synthetic decision log<br>    Given JOB-13 for at 02:00 on 3 December 2026<br>    Then the job run record shows type "JOB-14", business date 2 December 2026, status "COMPLETED" and result "INTACT"<br>    Then its checks hold entries checked 48,210, last chain_seq 48,210 and that entry's entry_hash<br>    Then MSG-P7-007 with the count and the last hash is sent to the Compliance group<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Break<br>    Given the actions of the entry with chain_seq 30,005 were changed in the SIT database with the trigger disabled<br>    When JOB-14 runs<br>    Then the run result is "BROKEN" with first break chain_seq 30,005 and its entry ID<br>    Then the system raises an incident and MSG-P7-008 with the entry ID is sent to the Compliance group before 08:00 on 3 December 2026<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 A run that stops is reported<br>    Given the database connection is closed in the test double after 20,000 entries<br>    When JOB-14 runs<br>    Then the run status is "FAILED" with entries checked 20,000<br>    Then the system alerts the platform administrator group<br>    Then the system sends MSG-P7-013 to the Compliance group with the date of the last complete check, 2 December 2026<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 A rerun gives a new record and no second scheduled run<br>    Given JOB-14 for business date 2 December 2026 failed at 02:20<br>    When test user "U-9301" holding RW-PLATADM reruns it from the job monitor at 08:30<br>    Then a second run record for business date 2 December 2026 is written with rerun true<br>    Then the system marks the report to Compliance as a rerun<br>    Then while the rerun is running a second start of JOB-14 is refused by the scheduler<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 An empty log is intact<br>    Given the decision log holds no entry<br>    When JOB-14 runs<br>    Then the result is "INTACT" with entries checked 0 and last hash of 64 zeros<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 The job waits for the decision log copy<br>    Given JOB-13 for business date 2 December 2026 is still running at 02:00 and ends at 02:25<br>    When JOB-14 fires at 02:00<br>    Then it starts the check at 02:25 and records the JOB-13 end time<br>    Then if JOB-13 has not ended by 04:00 the check starts at 04:00 with the note "JOB-13 not ended"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 Several changed entries<br>    Given the entries with chain_seq 12,001 and 40,350 were changed in the SIT database<br>    When JOB-14 runs<br>    Then the first break is chain_seq 12,001 and the failing count is 2<br>    Then one incident and one MSG-P7-008 report name both entry IDs<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Entries written during the run<br>    Given the log holds 48,210 entries when JOB-14 starts<br>    Given 37 entries are appended while it runs<br>    When the run ends<br>    Then entries checked is 48,210 and last chain_seq is 48,210<br>    Then the next night's run checks 48,247 entries |

and "rwb-quarantine"<br>    Given "rwb-docs-legal" has a time-based immutability policy of 3650 days<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Write-once<br>    Given court notice "DOC-0701" stored at "rwb-docs-legal/RW-2026-000701/DOC-0701/v1" with hash "H701"<br>    When a client using the ACE service managed identity tries to overwrite it<br>    Then storage refuses the write with "BlobImmutableDueToPolicy"<br>    Then the blob hash is still "H701"<br>    Then the attempt is in the audit log with the identity and the time<br>    Then DocumentStore.replaceContent for "DOC-0701" raises WriteOnceViolationException, shown as 409 with MSG-P5-002<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 Delete is refused<br>    Given court notice "DOC-0701" in "rwb-docs-legal"<br>    When a client using the ACE service managed identity tries to delete it<br>    Then storage refuses the delete<br>    Then the blob is still readable<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 A new blob in the write-once container is allowed<br>    Given court notice "DOC-0701" at "v1" in "rwb-docs-legal"<br>    When the ACE service identity writes "rwb-docs-legal/RW-2026-000701/DOC-0701/v2"<br>    Then the write succeeds and both "v1" and "v2" are readable<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 The standard container keeps earlier versions<br>    Given "rwb-docs/RW-2026-000702/DOC-0702/v1" holds an appraisal report<br>    When the blob is overwritten by the ACE service identity in SIT<br>    Then the previous blob version is still readable through blob versioning<br>    Then a deleted blob can be restored within the 30 days of soft delete<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 The identities hold only their roles<br>    Given the role assignments applied by the template in SIT<br>    When the assignments are listed<br>    Then the ACE service identity holds Storage Blob Data Contributor on "rwb-intake", "rwb-docs" and "rwb-docs-legal" only<br>    Then "RWB-SIT-SY-OCR" holds Storage Blob Data Contributor on "rwb-intake", "rwb-docs" and "rwb-quarantine" only<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 Key-based access is refused<br>    Given the storage account has shared key access disabled<br>    When a client calls "rwb-docs" with the account key or with a shared access signature<br>    Then storage refuses the request with "KeyBasedAuthenticationNotPermitted"<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 An identity with no role is refused<br>    Given managed identity "RWB-SIT-SY-AGENT" has no role on "rwb-docs-legal"<br>    When it tries to read "rwb-docs-legal/RW-2026-000701/DOC-0701/v1"<br>    Then storage refuses with "AuthorizationPermissionMismatch"<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Public network access is refused<br>    Given public network access is disabled on the storage account<br>    When a client outside the private endpoint calls the account<br>    Then the system refuses the request<br><br>  Scenario: AC9 A missing immutability policy stops the document hub<br>    Given the immutability policy is removed from "rwb-docs-legal" in DEV<br>    When the service starts<br>    Then the readiness check reports the document hub as not ready, naming "rwb-docs-legal"<br>    Then an upload through POST /api/recovery/v1/cases/{caseId}/documents returns 503 with MSG-GEN-006<br>    Then the system writes no document row<br><br>  Scenario: AC10 Storage does not answer<br>    Given the storage account does not answer within 10 seconds<br>    When BlobStorageAdapter writes a file to "rwb-docs"<br>    Then it raises StorageUnavailableException after 10 seconds<br>    Then the upload endpoint returns 503 with MSG-GEN-006<br><br>  Scenario: AC11 The quarantine container is closed to user sessions<br>    Given file "Q-0001" in "rwb-quarantine"<br>    When the ACE service tries to read it on behalf of a case owner<br>    Then the system refuses the read<br>    Then only "RWB-SIT-SY-OCR" can write to "rwb-quarantine"<br><br>  Scenario: AC12 A repeated write of the same file is not an overwrite<br>    Given "rwb-docs-legal/RW-2026-000703/DOC-0703/v1" holds a file with hash "H703"<br>    When BlobStorageAdapter is asked to write the same file with hash "H703" to the same name<br>    Then the system attempts no overwrite and the existing blob reference is returned<br>    Then the same name with a different hash raises WriteOnceViolationException |
| RWB-TE-013 | Agent runtime client with fallback | Feature: Agent runtime client with fallback<br><br>  Background:<br>    Given agent "INTAKE" has approved policy "P1" with timeout 60 seconds and prompt "PR1"<br>    Given case "RW-2026-000201" is in route state "NEW" and status "New"<br>    Given the calling step has rule result rule "R-UNI-32" version "2026.11" with next state "RESUME_STATE"<br><br>  Scenario: AC1 Shadow<br>    Given the policy of "INTAKE" has autonomy mode "SHADOW"<br>    When the agent answers in 2,000 ms with a valid output<br>    Then the system stores the agent answer on the agent run and the rule result is the applied result<br>    Then one decision log entry holds agent version "INTAKE@P1/PR1", the rule ID and version, recommendation null and fallback false in actions<br>    Then the calling step receives the rule result and no agent output<br><br>  Scenario: AC2 No answer<br>    Given the agent does not answer in 60 seconds<br>    When the step runs<br>    Then the system uses the rule result and the step continues to "RESUME_STATE"<br>    Then the decision log entry holds actions fallback true with reason "TIMED_OUT" and the agent run ID (R-UNI-32)<br>    Then the agent run holds status "TIMED_OUT" and fallback true<br><br>  Scenario: AC3 Assisted mode returns the answer as a recommendation<br>    Given the policy of "INTAKE" has autonomy mode "L1"<br>    When the agent answers with confidence 0.91<br>    Then the rule result is the applied result<br>    Then the outcome returns the agent answer as the recommendation for the person who decides<br>    Then the decision log entry holds the agent answer in recommendation with confidence 0.91<br><br>  Scenario: AC4 A guardrail failure falls back<br>    Given the agent answer fails check "SCHEMA"<br>    When the step runs<br>    Then the system applies the rule result<br>    Then the decision log entry holds actions fallback true with reason "SCHEMA"<br><br>  Scenario: AC5 No approved policy<br>    Given agent "INTAKE" has no approved policy on 2026-11-30<br>    When the step runs on 2026-11-30<br>    Then the system makes no orchestrator call<br>    Then the system applies the rule result with fallback true and reason "NO_POLICY" in the decision log entry<br><br>  Scenario: AC6 The decision log write fails<br>    Given the decision log writer throws on write<br>    When the step runs<br>    Then the calling step does not act and case "RW-2026-000201" stays in "NEW"<br>    Then the client raises DecisionNotRecordedException carrying MSG-P7-003 and an incident is raised (R-UNI-39)<br>    Then the agent run stays with decision_log_id empty<br><br>  Scenario: AC7 A repeated step key<br>    Given the step with key "CASEWF:RW-2026-000201:ENTRY:2026-11-30" ran once<br>    When it runs again with the same key<br>    Then one agent run and one decision log entry exist for the key<br>    Then the second call returns the first outcome (R-UNI-40)<br><br>  Scenario: AC8 Agent switched off<br>    Given the policy of "INTAKE" has autonomy mode "OFF"<br>    When the step runs<br>    Then no orchestrator call and no agent run row are made<br>    Then the decision log entry holds agent version null and fallback false |
